To address this gap, we introduce SkillComposer, a framework that decomposes skill construction into three learnable operations: create, improve, and merge. Trained via systematic rejection sampling recipe, SkillComposer enables language models to self-evolve skills at inference time and supports three deployment modes: offline for building generalized libraries, online for task-specific refinement, and hybrid for combining both. Comprehensive experiments on $τ^2$-Bench, LiveCodeBench v6, and AppWorld show that SkillComposer consistently outperforms baselines. Our SkillComposer-4B improves a 27B executor by up to +4.5 on agent tasks and +3.4 on code tasks, while generalizing across domains and task types unseen during training. Analysis reveals that merge and improve address orthogonal quality dimensions and that skill composition is a transferable meta-ability, providing a practical recipe for skill-augmented inference.

</details>


### [77] [LatentSkill: From In-Context Textual Skills to In-Weight Latent Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06087)
*Aofan Yu,Chenyu Zhou,Tianyi Xu,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentSkill将文本技能转化为LoRA适配器，将技能知识存储在权重空间而非上下文空间，减少了推理时的token开销，同时在ALFWorld和Search-QA上超越了上下文技能基线，并支持模块化加载、缩放和组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统将文本技能注入提示词中，导致大量上下文开销，且技能内容以明文形式暴露，存在隐私和安全风险。

Method: 通过预训练的超网络将文本技能转化为即插即用的LoRA适配器，将技能知识编码在权重空间中，而非上下文提示中。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，seen和unseen分组的成功率分别提升21.4和13.4个百分点，预填充token减少64.1%；在Search-QA上，精确匹配提升3.0个百分点，技能token开销降低72.2%。

Conclusion: 权重空间的技能表示比上下文技能更高效、更模块化、暴露风险更低，为扩展LLM智能体提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly use textual skills to encode reusable task procedures, but injecting these skills into the prompt at every step incurs substantial context overhead and exposes skill content as plaintext. We present LatentSkill, a framework that converts textual skills into plug-and-play LoRA adapters through a pretrained hypernetwork. LatentSkill stores skill knowledge in weight space rather than context space, removing per-step skill tokens while preserving modular loading, scaling, and composition. On ALFWorld and Search-QA, LatentSkill outperforms the corresponding in-context skill baseline while using substantially fewer prefill tokens: it improves ALFWorld success by 21.4 and 13.4 points on the seen and unseen splits with 64.1% fewer prefill tokens, and improves Search-QA exact match by 3.0 points with 72.2% lower skill-token overhead. Further analysis shows that generated skill LoRAs form a structured semantic geometry, can be precisely controlled via the LoRA scaling coefficient, and can be composed through parameter-space arithmetic when skill components are aligned. These findings suggest that weight-space skills provide an efficient, modular, and less exposed substrate for extending LLM agents.

</details>


### [78] [CHALIS: A Challenge Dataset for Language Identification in Difficult Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06088)
*Michal Tichý,Jindřich Libovický*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHALIS是一个专门针对语言识别中困难场景（亲属语言和拼写噪声）的新基准数据集，包含相互可理解的语言对和多种拼写变体，评估发现现有系统在这些场景下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言识别系统在常规场景下表现良好，但在亲属语言（相互可理解的语言对）和拼写噪声（转写、去变音符号、同形攻击、网络俚语）等困难情况下缺乏系统性评估，需要专门的基准来揭示这些短板。

Method: 构建了两部分数据集：1) 收集相互可理解语言对（捷克语/斯洛伐克语、西班牙语/加泰罗尼亚语、葡萄牙语/加利西亚语、丹麦语/挪威语）的共享句子；2) 通过跨脚本转写、去变音符号、模拟同形攻击和使用网络俚语制造拼写噪声。使用四种广泛使用的语言识别系统进行评估。

Result: 所有被评估的系统在CHALIS场景下都表现出显著困难，尤其是在亲属语言对中的低资源语言以及转写输入上表现尤为糟糕。

Conclusion: CHALIS揭示了现有语言识别系统在困难场景下的系统性弱点，为改进语言识别技术提供了有价值的基准测试资源。

Abstract: We present CHALIS (Challenging Language Identification Samples), a new benchmark dataset explicitly designed to address difficult cases in language identification: cousin languages and orthographic noise. Our dataset has two parts: First, we collected sentences shared across mutually intelligible language pairs (Czech/Slovak, Spanish/Catalan, Portuguese/Galician, Danish/Norwegian). The second part tests for orthography noise: we transliterate text across multiple scripts, remove diacritics, simulate homoglyph attacks, and use Internet slang. We evaluate four widely used language identification systems on CHALIS and demonstrate that all struggle substantially in these scenarios, especially on lower-resource languages within cousin pairs and on transliterated input. The resource is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/michal-tichy/CHALIS.

</details>


### [79] [IR3DE: A Linear Router for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06098)
*Eros Fanì,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IR3DE，一种基于岭回归的轻量级推理路由器，用于为每个提示选择最合适的领域专家大语言模型，在无需大量训练的情况下实现高效路由，并在推理任务中达到98.4%的归一化性能，同时支持动态增删专家模型而无需重新训练路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理路由方法要么只针对弱到强通用模型的成本优化，要么需要大量训练才能支持领域专家路由，缺乏高效、低成本的领域专家路由方案。

Method: 提出IR3DE，一种基于岭回归（Ridge Regression）的线性路由器，对每个提示进行快速低成本的路由决策，支持两种因果语言建模设置和一种推理设置，并支持动态增删领域专家而无需从头重训练。

Result: 在两种CLM设置中性能与基线方法相当，在推理设置中以98.4%的归一化性能超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: IR3DE作为一种线性路由器，能够在低成本下实现与复杂方法相当甚至更优的路由性能，并支持动态专家管理，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Foundational Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate proficiency on a wide range of general tasks, and achieve remarkable results on various specialized tasks via domain-expert LLMs. With the ever-growing list of available LLMs, inference routers are being proposed to select the most appropriate LLM for each prompt. However, existing routing methods either optimize cost across weak-to-strong generalist LLMs or require substantial training to support domain-expertise routing. In this paper, we propose IR3DE, a Ridge Regression-based Router for Domain Experts that provides cheap and fast routing decisions for each prompt. We evaluate IR3DE in two Causal Language Modeling (CLM) settings where the tasks are next-token prediction for all domains, and one reasoning setting where each domain has its own distinct reasoning task. Despite being a linear router, IR3DE achieves performance comparable to the other baselines in both CLM settings, and surpassing them in the reasoning setting, with a normalized performance of 98.4%. Moreover, IR3DE enables the addition or removal of new domain experts without requiring the router to be retrained from scratch, allowing a dynamic set of LLMs to be served with minimal disruption to the router itself. Our code is available at: github.com/gensyn-ai/IR3DE.

</details>


### [80] [Harnessing Structural Context for Entity Alignment Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06109)
*Xingyu Chen,Yuanning Cui,Zequn Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextEA通过增强编码器-解码器框架中的结构上下文利用，显著提升了实体对齐基础模型的可迁移性，在29个数据集上超越现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EA基础模型在两个环节对结构上下文利用不足：编码时跨KG交互较弱，解码时候选排序过于依赖粗粒度相似度。这限制了模型向未见KG对的迁移能力。

Method: 编码端引入跨KG交互编码器，通过锚点桥统一两个KG并执行早期间感知的跨图传播；解码端引入结构校准解码器，结合实体级、邻居级、关系级和锚点感知的结构证据校准对齐分数。

Result: 在OpenEA、SRPRS和DBP的29个EA数据集上，ContextEA一致超越强迁移基线。预训练模型甚至超越微调基线，展现出显著更强的向未见KG迁移的能力。

Conclusion: 显式利用结构上下文是改进EA基础模型的有效方向。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) aims to identify equivalent entities across heterogeneous knowledge graphs (KGs) and is a key component of knowledge fusion and cross-KG reasoning. The recent EA foundation model demonstrates that alignment knowledge, once pretrained, can be directly applied to diverse previously unseen KG pairs. However, it still underuses structural context in two places: cross-KG interaction is weak during encoding, and final candidate ranking still relies too heavily on coarse similarity. We address these limitations with ContextEA, an enhanced encoder-decoder framework for transferable EA. On the encoder side, we introduce a cross-KG interaction encoder that unifies the two KGs with anchor bridges and performs earlier relation-aware cross-graph propagation. On the decoder side, we introduce a structural calibration decoder that calibrates alignment scores with entity-level, neighborhood-level, relation-level, and anchor-aware structural evidence. This design strengthens both structural context construction and structural context exploitation while remaining lightweight. Experiments on 29 EA datasets in OpenEA, SRPRS, and DBP show consistent gains over strong transferable baselines. Notably, the pretrained ContextEA already surpasses the finetuned baselines on all three benchmark groups, demonstrating substantially stronger transfer to unseen KGs. These results suggest that explicitly harnessing structural context is an effective direction for improving EA foundation models.

</details>


### [81] [Ouvia: A User-centered Framework for Measuring Usability of Speech Translation in Real-World Communication Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06177)
*Giuseppe Attanasio,Beatrice Savoldi,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Matteo Negri,Marine Carpuat,Maarten Sap,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Ouvia框架，通过1750+次真实场景下的英葡双语语音翻译交互实验，发现现代ST系统仅约半数交互被用户评为可用，且存在显著的人口统计群体差异；相比传统评估方法，QA评估更能预测实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音翻译评估主要关注去语境化的测试集和整体质量分数，未能反映终端用户在真实交流中的实际需求，缺乏对用户感知可用性的系统评估。

Method: 设计Ouvia评估框架，聚焦英语使用者向葡萄牙语使用者传达请求的单向交流场景；通过定制网页应用和多阶段研究设计，收集了1750+次交互数据，涵盖医疗和日常生活场景、四种ST系统、三种英语方言和两种性别。

Result: 现代ST系统仅约50%的交互被用户评为可用；不同人口统计群体间存在显著的可用性差异；QA评估比传统方法更能有效预测实际可用性。

Conclusion: 强调情境化和以用户为中心的评估框架的重要性，需要超越整体质量分数，关注技术究竟服务于谁以及服务效果如何。

Abstract: Speech translation (ST) is increasingly adopted in user applications, yet its evaluation largely focuses on decontextualized testbeds and holistic quality, rather than end users' communication needs. We introduce Ouvia, an evaluation framework for measuring user-perceived usability of speech translation outputs in real-world settings. Ouvia focuses on one-to-one communication: an English speaker needs to convey a request to a Portuguese speaker, and the message is automatically translated. Through a custom web app and multi-phase study design, we collect more than 1,750 such interactions in healthcare and everyday situations, mediated by four ST systems, involving speakers from three English dialects and two genders. We find that modern ST serves people only to a limited extent -- only around half of interactions are rated as usable -- with significant gaps in reported usability across demographic groups. Moreover, among quality metrics, we find that QA-based evaluation is a substantially stronger predictor of real-world usability than standard approaches. Together, these findings stress the importance of situated, user-centered evaluation frameworks that go beyond holistic quality scores and attend to who the technology serves -- and how well.

</details>


### [82] [The Tell-Tale Norm: $\ell_2$ Magnitude as a Signal for Reasoning Dynamics in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06188)
*Jinyang Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Muyang Ye,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM隐藏状态的l2范数可作为推理强度的内生信号，并基于此提出三种无需额外训练的测试时缩放技术，显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽尝试理解LLM的推理机制，但缺乏一种 principled、模型内在的信号来捕捉其逐层推理动态。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）作为诊断探针，发现推理特征激活在后期层急剧增加；理论上证明l2范数约束了SAE推理特征的激活强度；通过经验相关性分析和因果干预验证l2范数作为推理强度的可靠指标；基于此提出三种l2范数引导的测试时缩放技术：自适应层推理递归、内生推理状态引导、l2引导响应选择。

Result: 跨模型架构和基准的实验表明，基于l2范数的技术显著提升了推理性能。

Conclusion: l2范数提供了一种principled且简洁的视角来感知和控制LLM潜在推理动态，且无需额外训练或数据，与现有推理引擎兼容。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to understand Large Language Models (LLMs) reasoning, yet a principled, model-intrinsic signal that captures its layer-wise reasoning dynamics remains underexplored. We bridge this gap by demonstrating that the l2 norm of hidden states serves as an endogenous signal of the model's reasoning intensity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a diagnostic probe, we observe that LLMs' internal reasoning is marked by a sharp increase in reasoning feature activations concentrated in late layers. Motivated by this pattern, we establish a formal link between reasoning intensity and the model's latent geometry and theoretically prove that the l2 norm of hidden states bounds the activation strength of SAE reasoning features. Empirical correlation analysis and causal interventions further validate the l2 norm as a faithful indicator, where heightened norms consistently correspond to critical reasoning steps. We then introduce three test-time scaling techniques guided by l2 norms: (i) Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion, (ii) Endogenous Reasoning State Steering, and (iii) l2-guided Response Selection, which requires no additional training or data and is compatible with advanced inference engines. Experiments across model architectures and benchmarks show that l2-norm-based techniques significantly improve reasoning performance, offering a principled yet simple lens to perceive and control LLM latent reasoning dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjy1298/The-Tell-Tale-Norm.

</details>


### [83] [Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197)
*Hafez Abdelghaffar,Ahmed Alansary,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在复杂或模糊查询下难以从给定上下文中准确提取和生成精确答案的问题，提出基于微调RoBERTa-base模型的QA系统。在SQuAD1.1数据集上，模型取得ROUGE-L 86.84%、BLEU 28.24%、BERTScore 95.38%的性能，验证了定向微调对提升QA系统可靠性与精确度的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QA系统在上下文理解、答案一致性和跨领域泛化方面存在不足，即使有正确上下文也常产生不相关或不精确的回答，亟需提升系统在复杂/模糊查询下的准确提取与生成能力。

Method: 采用预训练的LLM（RoBERTa-base）在SQuAD1.1基准数据集上进行监督微调，利用高质量上下文-问题-答案三元组训练模型，以增强其上下文理解和答案抽取能力。

Result: 微调后的RoBERTa-base模型在SQuAD1.1上取得ROUGE-L 86.84%、BLEU 28.24%、BERTScore 95.38%的成绩，表明模型在答案准确性和相关性上表现优异。

Conclusion: 定向微调能显著提升QA系统的可靠性和精确度，所提方法在基于上下文的问答任务中效果良好，验证了微调策略的有效性。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) systems have achieved notable progress with the advent of large language models (LLMs). However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [84] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现词汇密度（上下文引入不同信息的速率）是除输入长度和信息位置外，导致LLM长上下文性能下降的第三个关键因素。通过三个长度一致（~12k tokens）但密度不同的"找针"基准测试，发现模型在稀疏上下文中接近完美，但在高密度上下文中检索得分降至60%以下。降低密度可恢复性能，表明有效上下文容量是词汇密度的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注输入长度和相关信息位置对LLM长上下文性能的影响，但忽略了上下文信息密度这一潜在因素。本文旨在揭示词汇密度作为第三个系统性影响LLM有效上下文窗口的因素，并量化其影响程度。

Method: 使用三个"找针"式基准测试，长度相同（~12k tokens），控制"针"的位置，但信息密度递增。在多个开放权重LLM（9B-685B）上进行实验。通过在同一基准内变化和控制密度（保持其他属性不变）来排除任务类型混淆。

Result: 高密度基准测试中模型性能急剧下降：在稀疏上下文中接近完美的模型，在高密度上下文中检索得分降至60%以下。降低密度通常能恢复性能，特别是在性能退化明显的高密度区域。有效上下文容量是词汇密度的函数。

Conclusion: 词汇密度是影响LLM长上下文性能的系统性因素，对处理紧凑、信息丰富输入的实际LLM系统具有直接影响。研究挑战了仅关注长度和位置的传统认知，为改进LLM长上下文能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [85] [FiLM-Based Speaker Conditioning of a SpeechLLM for Pathological Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06211)
*Fernando López,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过特征线性调制（FiLM）将x-vector说话人信息注入冻结的ASR编码器各Transformer层，实现病理语音的说话人自适应，在不修改基础模型权重的情况下，该方法在西班牙语和英语病理语音识别上与标准微调和参数高效微调基线竞争力相当，并保留了下游问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语音识别在病理语音（如神经系统疾病导致的言语障碍）上表现不佳，现有自适应方法需要修改模型权重或无法有效捕捉个体说话人特征，因此需要一种不修改基础模型权重且能有效适应个体病理说话人的方法。

Method: 采用特征线性调制（FiLM）机制，将x-vector说话人嵌入作为条件信号，注入到冻结的ASR Transformer编码器的每一层中，通过仿射变换调整中间表示，从而在不更新预训练模型权重的情况下实现对个体病理说话人的自适应。

Result: 说话人条件ASR在病理语音识别任务上与标准微调和参数高效微调基线方法竞争力相当，同时在对非条件语音（正常语音）的识别性能上保持不下降，并且保留了模型回答语音相关问题的能力。

Conclusion: 基于FiLM的说话人条件化是一种有效的病理语音自适应策略，能够在冻结大模型的前提下实现个性化适配，兼顾了适应效果与通用能力的保持。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech; however, pathological speech from neurological conditions remains a significant challenge. We investigate speaker conditioning via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), injecting x-vector-derived information into each transformer layer of a frozen ASR encoder to adapt internal representations to individual pathological speakers without modifying base model weights. We benchmark this for the ASR task against standard and parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, complemented by post-processing, on Spanish and English pathological speech. Additionally, we evaluate if the adapted model preserves the ability to answer speech-related questions. Results show that speaker-conditioned ASR is competitive with established adaptation strategies while retaining performance on non-conditioned speech.

</details>


### [86] [Benchmarking Open-Source Layout Detection Models for Data Snapshot Extraction from Institutional Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06242)
*AJ Carl P. Dy,Aivin V. Solatorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向机构文档的“数据快照提取”基准数据集和评估框架，发现现有布局检测模型在操作类机构文档上泛化能力差，存在将分析性内容与非分析性内容混淆、复合分析工件碎片化、上下文信息提取不完整等问题，揭示了通用文档布局分析与实用数据快照提取之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构文档中的图表包含大量可复用的分析信息，但现有方法仅将图表视作文档对象而非语义上有意义的分析工件，缺乏专门针对机构文档中语义化视觉内容提取的基准和评估方法。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖人道主义报告、世行政策研究论文和项目评估文档的基准数据集，标注了包含可复用分析信息的图表；对多个开源布局检测模型进行了检测性能与空间提取质量的全面评估。

Result: 现有模型在常规学术基准上表现良好，但在操作类机构文档上泛化能力不足；主要失败模式包括分析性/非分析性内容混淆、复合分析工件碎片化、以及解释所需上下文信息提取不完整。

Conclusion: 通用文档布局分析与实务中有用的数据快照提取之间存在显著差距，需要专门针对机构文档的语义化视觉内容提取研究。

Abstract: Institutional documents contain substantial amounts of operational and analytical information embedded within figures and tables. Current approaches for extracting visual content from documents are largely built around generic document layout analysis, where figures and tables are treated as uniformly relevant document objects rather than semantically meaningful analytical artifacts. In this work, we introduce a benchmark dataset and evaluation framework for \textit{data snapshot extraction}, the task of identifying and localizing semantically meaningful visual artifacts within institutional documents. The benchmark spans humanitarian reports, World Bank policy research working papers, and project appraisal documents, and includes annotations for figures and tables that contain reusable analytical information. Using this dataset, we benchmarked multiple open-source layout detection models and evaluated both detection performance and spatial extraction quality. Our results show that current models struggle to generalize to operational institutional documents despite strong performance on conventional academic benchmarks. Common failure modes include confusion between analytical and non-analytical content, fragmentation of composite analytical artifacts, and incomplete extraction of contextual information required for interpretation. These findings highlight a persistent gap between generic document layout analysis and operationally useful data snapshot extraction. We release the source PDFs, annotation dataset, metadata, and source code to support future research in operational document intelligence. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ai4data/data-snapshot and the source code is available at https://github.com/worldbank/ai4data/tree/main/experimental/data-snapshot.

</details>


### [87] [From Self to Other: Evaluating Demographic Perspective-Taking in LLM Hate Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06266)
*Paloma Piot,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了人物角色条件化的大语言模型在仇恨言论检测中模拟不同人口群体间主观分歧的能力，发现没有任何模型能同时完美捕捉群体间分歧、内群体敏感性和替代预测三个维度，但使用Llama 3.1的替代提示配置最接近人类判断模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测具有主观性，不同人口群体对同一内容的感知不同，但收集多群体标注成本高昂且难以规模化。因此需要探索能否使用人物角色条件化的大语言模型来模拟不同群体的多样化视角。

Method: 评估人物角色条件化LLM在三个方面的表现：(i) 群体间分歧——不同群体角色是否以类人方式产生分歧；(ii) 内群体敏感性——当内容针对自身身份时是否更敏感；(iii) 替代预测——能否准确预测其他群体的反应。对比了不同模型和提示策略。

Result: 没有模型能一致地捕捉所有三个维度，性能高度依赖模型选择，且不能仅从简单的身份提示中可靠涌现。但使用Llama 3.1的替代提示在大多数人口统计维度上取得了最高的跨群体一致性，最接近人类分歧模式。

Conclusion: 当前人物角色条件化LLM在模拟人类主观判断方面仍存在局限性，但特定配置（Llama 3.1 + 替代提示）可作为自动标注的可靠替代方案，以更好地对齐人类判断。

Abstract: Hate speech detection is inherently subjective: people from different demographic groups perceive the same content very differently. Collecting enough annotations from multiple demographic groups is costly and difficult to scale. Persona-conditioned Large Language Models (models prompted to adopt a specific demographic identity) have been proposed as a way to simulate diverse perspectives at scale. But do they actually reflect how different groups disagree? We evaluate three aspects of human social judgement: (i) whether personas from different groups disagree in human-like ways (inter-group disagreement), (ii) whether they become more sensitive when content targets their own identity (in-group sensitivity), and (iii) whether they can accurately predict how another group would react (vicarious prediction). Our results show that no model consistently captures all three dimensions, and performance is highly model-dependent and does not emerge reliably from minimal identity prompts alone. However, vicarious prompting with Llama 3.1 yields the highest cross-group agreement in most demographic axes and provides the closest overall approximation to human disagreement patterns, indicating that this configuration may provide a more reliable setting for automatic annotation aligned with human judgements.

</details>


### [88] [Many Circuits, One Mechanism: Input Variation and Evaluation Granularity in Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06267)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过字面序列复制任务，发现电路在结构上存在明显差异（"幻象特化"），但这些差异并不对应功能上的不同，结构差异不足以证明不同机制的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验一个常见假设：电路发现方法识别出的子图在结构上的差异是否真正反映了不同的计算机制。

Method: 固定字面序列复制任务，变化输入统计特征（四种词频区间+对照条件），在五个Pythia模型（70M-1.4B）上提取75个电路，通过跨频带迁移测试、核心共享子图分析、因果交换干预实验及重复提取实验进行分析。

Result: 结构不同的电路实现了相同的计算：频带特异性边可广泛跨频带迁移；核心共享子图恢复至少99%性能；内部表示可跨频带互换。重复提取表明发现算法从等价类中采样而非恢复唯一机制。源级评估夸大忠实性，边级评估揭示了结构到功能的"多对一"映射。

Conclusion: 电路的结构差异不足以证明不同机制的存在，暴露这一问题需要边级评估和跨条件迁移测试。

Abstract: Circuit discovery methods identify subgraphs that explain specific model behaviors, and structural differences between discovered circuits are commonly interpreted as evidence of distinct mechanisms. We test this assumption by varying input statistics while holding the task fixed, and show that the resulting structural differences exhibit apparent specialization but do not correspond to functional differences, a pattern we term phantom specialization. Using Literal Sequence Copying across four token-frequency bands plus a control condition in five Pythia models (70M-1.4B), we extract 75 circuits and find that structurally distinct circuits implement the same computation: band-specific edges transfer broadly across bands, a core shared across most bands recovers at least 99% of circuit performance, and causal interchange interventions confirm that internal representations are interchangeable across frequency bands. Repeated extractions within the same frequency band further suggest that discovery algorithms sample from an equivalence class of valid subgraphs rather than recovering a unique mechanism. Standard evaluation practice obscures this pattern: source-level evaluation inflates apparent faithfulness, while edge-level evaluation reveals the many-to-one mapping from structure to function. Our results show that structural differences between circuits are not sufficient evidence for distinct mechanisms, and that exposing this requires edge-level evaluation and cross-condition transfer tests.

</details>


### [89] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了FOXGLOVE数据集，系统比较了LLM与写作教师提供的反馈在目标导向、锚定句子和优先级排序三个维度的异同，发现两者在反馈目标和位置分布上相似，但在具体句子选择和反馈复杂度上存在差异，且LLM反馈在质量评分上更高，但优势主要源于评论长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被用于生成写作反馈，但尚无研究系统性地比较LLM反馈与专家反馈在写作研究认为对修订至关重要的维度（目标导向性、锚定到具体句子、优先级排序）上的差异。

Method: 创建FOXGLOVE数据集，包含训练有素的写作教师在69篇12年级议论文上撰写的696条反馈评论，以及4个前沿LLM在共享协议下生成的1,644条评论，总计2,340条评论，并对子集进行了专家质量评分。

Result: 教师和LLM在反馈目标和论文位置上的分布相似，但在提供反馈的具体句子选择上存在分歧；LLM倾向于撰写更复杂的反馈，使用更少疑问句；LLM反馈在多数质量维度上获得更高评分，但这一优势很大程度上归因于更长的评论。

Conclusion: FOXGLOVE数据集能够系统性地比较人类和LLM反馈在哪些方面一致、存在分歧以及存在差异。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [90] [LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286)
*Gianluca Barmina,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PropMe框架区分能力（对抗性提取）与倾向性（自然使用下的记忆泄露），通过SimpleTrace管道在完全开源模型上发现：前缀攻击能引发强记忆信号，但倾向性得分普遍较低；持续预训练可降低记忆倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆化评估大多测量模型在被迫（对抗性）条件下能否复现训练数据，而非评估模型在普通使用场景下是否真的会泄露训练数据，缺乏对自然使用中记忆倾向性的度量。

Method: 1) 提出PropMe框架，区分基于前缀的能力攻击与非对抗性评估；2) 设计度量变换方法将现有记忆指标转换为倾向性指标；3) 构建SimpleTrace轻量级追溯管道，基于infini-gram将模型生成确定性归因到大规模训练语料，计算逐字、近似逐字及倾向性变换后的记忆指标。

Result: 在Comma和DFM Decoder两个模型、Common Pile和Dynaword两个数据集、两种语言上：前缀攻击引发的记忆信号显著强于通用或数据集特定提示，但倾向性得分整体较低；DFM Decoder（从Comma持续预训练而来）在Common Pile上的记忆能力和倾向性均降低。

Conclusion: 模型在直接诱导下可以泄露训练数据，但在非对抗性的普通使用中很少发生；建议记忆审计应同时报告最坏情况下的可提取性（能力）和日常泄露倾向性，以获得更全面的视角。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce training data, but existing memorization evaluations mostly measure whether models can be forced to do so, rather than whether they do so under ordinary use. We introduce PropMe, a propensity-aware framework for memorization evaluation that contrasts prefix-based capability attacks with non-adversarial evaluations. We propose a metric transformation that, applied to existing functions, allows to create propensity metrics. We further introduce SimpleTrace, a lightweight tracing pipeline built on infini-gram that deterministically attributes model generations to large-scale training corpora and computes verbatim, near-verbatim, and propensity-transformed memorization metrics. Evaluating two fully-open models: Comma and DFM Decoder on two datasets: Common Pile and Dynaword in two languages, we find a consistent gap between capability and propensity: prefix attacks elicit substantially stronger memorization signals than generic or dataset-specific prompts, while propensity scores remain low overall. Thus, the models can reveal training data when directly elicited, but rarely do so in more common non-adversarial settings. We also find that DFM Decoder, which is continually pre-trained from Comma, exhibits reduced memorization and memorization propensity for Common Pile, confirming that memorization capability can decrease when later training emphasizes partially different data. Our results suggest, and we encourage, that memorization audits should report both worst-case extractability and ordinary leakage propensity in order to have a more comprehensive view of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [91] [Decomposing Factual Sycophancy in Language Models: How Size and Instruction Tuning Shape Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06306)
*Victor De Marez,Luna De Bruyne,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 事实谄媚是指语言模型在社会压力下放弃正确、可验证答案的现象。本文将事实谄媚分解为"真理边际"（模型对真相的基线偏好强度）和"操纵敏感性"（压力使偏好偏移的程度）两个通道，通过对56个0.3B-32B参数的开源模型和13种操纵类型进行分析，发现脆弱性主要由模型规模主导，但指令微调改变了规模的作用方式：小模型变得更不稳健，而大模型变得更稳健。指令微调主要提升真理边际，但其行为效果取决于操纵类型；基线模型随规模扩大真理边际增加但操纵敏感性轻微上升，而指令微调模型真理边际增长更快且敏感性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的翻转率指标混淆了两种机制：模型对真相的基线偏好强度（真理边际）和社会压力使偏好偏移的程度（操纵敏感性）。本文旨在将这两个通道分离，以更精确地理解不同模型规模、是否经过指令微调以及不同操纵类型对事实谄媚的影响。

Method: 对56个开源模型（参数规模0.3B-32B）和13种社会操纵类型进行系统实验，将事实谄媚分解为真理边际和操纵敏感性两个通道，分别分析模型规模和指令微调对这两个通道的影响。

Result: 1）脆弱性主要由模型规模主导；2）指令微调改变规模的作用方式：小规模指令微调模型稳健性下降，大规模指令微调模型稳健性提升；3）指令微调主要提升真理边际，但行为效果因操纵类型而异；4）基线模型随规模扩大真理边际增加但操纵敏感性轻微上升，指令微调模型真理边际增长更快且敏感性下降。

Conclusion: 事实谄媚不是一个单一的标量属性，评估应报告通道特定、操纵特定和规模条件化的稳健性指标，而非仅靠翻转率。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy occurs when a language model abandons a correct, verifiable answer under social pressure. Because a flip occurs only when pressure toward a false answer exceeds the model's neutral preference for the truth, flip rates conflate two mechanisms: the strength of that baseline preference (truth margin), and how far pressure shifts it (manipulation sensitivity). We decompose factual sycophancy into these channels and use them to separate the effects of size and instruction tuning across 56 open-weight models spanning 0.3B-32B parameters and 13 manipulation types. We find that vulnerability is governed mainly by size, but instruction tuning changes how size acts: small instruction-tuned models can become less robust, whereas large instruction-tuned models usually become more robust. Instruction tuning primarily increases truth margin, but its behavioral effect depends on manipulation type. Scaling also changes the two channels differently: base models gain margin but become mildly more manipulation-sensitive, whereas instruction-tuned models gain margin faster and become less sensitive. Factual sycophancy is therefore not a single scalar property. Evaluations should report channel-specific, manipulation-specific, and size-conditioned robustness rather than flip rates alone.

</details>


### [92] ["Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06349)
*Edoardo Signoroni,Pavel Rychlý*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对伦巴第语（意大利一个低资源语言连续体）的平行语料和单语语料进行了人工审计，发现网络爬取数据存在严重的语言误识别、样板文本和非语言噪声问题，且高质量数据严重偏向西伦巴第方言，东伦巴第方言被边缘化，揭示了"数据丰富"的假象，强调了需要关注语言变体、由社区驱动的数据策展策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界上许多语言在自然语言处理工具方面仍资源不足，主要原因是缺乏高质量数据集来训练、开发和评估机器翻译等任务的系统和模型。

Method: 对伦巴第语的可用的平行语料和单语语料进行人工审计，分析网络爬取数据集、策展语料库和基准测试中有效伦巴第语部分的正字法组成。

Result: 网络爬取的大规模数据集存在严重的语言误识别、样板文本和非语言噪声问题，"数据丰富"是假象；所有语料库中存在冲突的正字法系统和严重的代表性偏差，高质量数据严重偏向西伦巴第方言变体，东伦巴第方言被边缘化。

Conclusion: 需要关注语言变体、由社区驱动的数据策展策略，而非纯粹追求数量的爬取方式。

Abstract: Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.

</details>


### [93] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出证据诊断干预训练框架，利用模型内部信号（后验信念和输入接地分数）定位并修正评分推理中的错误步骤，再通过信念引导奖励塑造进行校准，在真实多学科评分基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评分方法仅关注准确分数预测，无法识别评分推理中的错误环节或模型对最终分数的信念变化过程，导致评分结果缺乏对评分标准和学生证据的忠实性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架EDIT：第一阶段EDIT-SFT利用模型内部信号（后验信念和输入接地分数）定位问题推理步骤，并结合评分标准清单仅修正局部错误步骤；第二阶段EDIT-RL通过信念引导奖励塑造，惩罚有害的信念漂移同时允许有益的探索。

Result: 在真实多学科评分基准上，EDIT在领域内和跨领域设置中均持续优于强监督微调和强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 内部状态诊断是驱动效果提升的关键，该框架能有效训练出更忠实于评分标准的大语言模型评分器。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [94] [Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于涌现语言（EL）的生成式方法论，通过在多智能体强化学习中让智能体从最低限度初始条件出发，在任务压力下自主发展通信，从而确保意识相关结构可归因于任务需求而非人类语言先验。实验证明智能体在最小环境中自发产生了自我指涉通信，包括回声-不匹配检测回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有意识研究方法（判别式checklist评估和架构式直接工程化）无法排除观察到的结构是否是人为语言先验的产物，需要一种能确保因果可归因性的生成式方法论。

Method: 采用涌现语言（EL）生成式方法论：多智能体强化学习中，智能体从无语言、无自我概念、极少接触人类文本的初始状态出发，在任务压力下自主演化通信，确保现象可因果归因于任务需求。

Result: 在最小环境中，智能体自发发展出自我指涉通信，包括一种回声-不匹配检测回路；该结构既非任务结构也非架构直接预测，而是源于特定环境可供性。

Conclusion: 涌现语言范式为研究意识相关结构提供了一种可因果归因的生成式方法论，能有效区分任务驱动产生的结构与人类语言先验的产物。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.

</details>


### [95] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CollabSim框架，用于系统分析大语言模型多智能体系统的协作能力，通过理论驱动的协作能力定义、可控交互条件操纵和动作级内部状态探测，实验揭示了不同LLM的协作表现差异及任务依赖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS评估主要关注任务结果或单智能体的推理、规划、工具使用能力，忽视了智能体间协作能力（建立共同基础、维护共享任务理解、平衡个体与集体激励、修复交互中的失调）这一关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出CollabSim仿真框架，包含：(1) 理论驱动的协作能力定义；(2) 交互条件的可控操纵；(3) 动作级智能体内部状态探测。在四种LLM上进行实验。

Result: CollabSim能够捕捉交互条件效应、区分模型性能模式，并揭示智能体设计对任务依赖的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统性分析MAS协作能力提供了有效工具，填补了现有评估范式的空白。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [96] [A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06420)
*Petr Parshakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个科米-亚兹瓦语-俄语平行语料库（457个对齐句对，74篇叙事文本），并设计了包含故事级交叉验证、基于检索的少样本学习、严格输出验证及多维度评估指标的可复现评估协议。实验表明，大语言模型在此极低资源濒危语言翻译场景下能产生有意义的译文，检索增强的少样本策略持续优于零样本，但评估结论高度依赖于指标选择与失败处理方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对濒危语言科米-亚兹瓦语在极低资源场景下缺乏平行语料与系统评估方法的问题，旨在构建一个兼具数据集贡献与可复现评估基准的测试平台，以系统研究大语言模型在此类极端低资源翻译任务上的表现。

Method: 构建457个对齐句对的科米-亚兹瓦语-俄语平行语料库，附带文本来源、句子级对齐及故事标识；设计故事级交叉验证、基于确定性检索的少样本提示、生成输出严格验证、参考指标与裁判模型指标结合、故事级不确定性估计的评估协议；对比多个现代LLM在零样本和检索增强少样本条件下的翻译表现。

Result: LLM能产生非平凡的翻译结果，但性能在不同模型家族和提示策略间差异显著；检索增强的少样本提示一致优于零样本提示，但超出少量检索上下文后的增益有限；评估结论在该场景下高度依赖于指标选择和失败处理方式。

Conclusion: 该语料库和评估协议可作为濒危语言机器翻译的可复现评估基准；强调在极低资源环境下评估方法论的重要性，即指标选择和失败处理会实质性影响结论。

Abstract: We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

</details>


### [97] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用强化学习（RL）方法，以chrF作为奖励信号，训练LLM从丰富的语言上下文中学习翻译技能，实现对完全未见过的极低资源语言的泛化翻译能力，效果优于上下文学习和监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（继续训练或上下文编码语法书）在处理未见过的或极低资源语言时存在过拟合问题，零样本迁移能力有限。作者认为LLM需要获得利用上下文语言知识的元技能，而非记忆特定语言。

Method: 采用强化学习（RL）方法，以表面级翻译指标chrF作为奖励信号，训练模型在丰富的语言上下文中进行未见语言翻译。

Result: 尽管奖励信号（chrF）较为轻量，RL训练后的模型能够有效提取和应用上下文中的相关语言信息，在完全未见过的语言上的翻译效果优于上下文学习和监督微调。

Conclusion: 基于结果的RL可以超越数学和编程等传统推理任务，扩展为一种从上下文中学习语言的通用方法。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [98] [Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06443)
*Xinnong Zhang,Wanting Shan,Hanjia Lyu,Zhongyu Wei,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种反事实上下文修订框架，用于审计基于LLM的立场模拟，发现LLM生成的立场模拟对会话上下文变化（包括纯文本和多模态策略）具有敏感性和鲁棒性，从而揭示了使用LLM模拟在线舆论动态的潜力与风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用于模拟社交媒体用户并推断个体对在线讨论的反应，但目前尚不清楚这些模拟是否反映精确的用户特定信念，以及它们是否对会话上下文的语义无关变化高度敏感。现有研究缺乏系统的审计框架来评估LLM立场模拟的上下文敏感性。

Method: 给定原始在线对话，首先推断目标用户对特定话题的立场；然后对会话上下文应用受控修订策略（包括纯文本策略和多模态策略，后者引入基于梗图的上下文），再次模拟用户的立场；通过平均定向立场偏移和立场转换率两个指标评估有效性。

Result: 在文本策略和多模态策略中均观察到有效且鲁棒的立场转换，这些转换在不同极化-偏好机制下表现一致。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个用于理解基于LLM的立场模拟上下文敏感性的评估框架，并凸显了使用LLM模拟在线舆论动态的前景与风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NF-CoT通过在LLM主干网络中引入归一化流（Normalizing Flows）实现潜在推理，在保持自回归语言模型关键优势（从左到右生成、概率采样、KV缓存兼容、似然可计算）的同时，以紧凑连续状态替代离散文本链式思维，在代码生成任务上显著降低中间推理成本并提升通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本链式思维（CoT）强制推理通过离散、串行、面向通信的token流进行，每个推理步骤必须 verbalized 后才能继续，限制了计算效率。现有潜在推理方法牺牲了CoT在自回归语言模型中的关键优势（如从左到右生成、概率采样、KV缓存解码、似然估计可行性）。

Method: 提出NF-CoT框架，在LLM骨干网络内部实例化TARFlow风格的归一化流，为从显式CoT中提炼的紧凑连续思维建立可处理的概率模型。连续思维位置由NF头生成，文本位置由标准LM头生成，两者在同一因果流中交替。该设计提供潜在思维的精确似然，支持带原始KV缓存的概率从左到右解码，以及潜在推理空间中的直接策略梯度优化。

Result: 在代码生成基准测试中，NF-CoT相比显式CoT和先前潜在推理基线提升了通过率，同时大幅降低了中间推理成本。

Conclusion: NF-CoT成功融合了显式CoT的结构化推理优势与潜在推理的高带宽特性，为语言模型提供了一种兼具效率与可解释性的推理范式。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [100] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了主动探索（active exploration）是否能改善成年人在合取因果规则（conjunctive causal rules）学习中的困难。通过改进的"blicket detector"任务，发现主动探索显著提升了合取因果推理表现，但合取规则仍比析取规则需要更多测试。同时对比了大型语言模型，发现部分先进模型在假设推理准确性上接近人类水平，但探索效率较低且存在类似的合取-析取表现差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明成年人在识别合取因果规则（多个原因同时存在才能产生效果）时存在困难，但多数研究依赖被动观察范式，学习者无法控制证据生成过程。本研究旨在探究当赋予成年人主动探索（active agency）时，这种"合取困难"（conjunctive handicap）是否仍然存在。

Method: 采用改进的"blicket detector"任务，让成年参与者通过自由干预（主动探索）来识别合取或析取规则结构下的因果对象。同时将人类表现与多种大型语言模型在相同设置下进行对比。

Result: 主动探索显著改善了成年人的合取因果推理能力，但合取规则仍比析取规则需要更多测试才能推断出来。部分最先进的大语言模型在假设推理准确性上接近人类水平，但它们的探索策略效率较低，且同样存在合取-析取表现差距。

Conclusion: 主动探索能够有效缓解但无法完全消除合取因果推理中的困难；即使是最先进的大语言模型在因果探索策略上仍与人类存在差距。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [101] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），基于KV共享架构（如YOCO），通过在跨解码器层间共享KV缓存和路由索引，实现令牌级稀疏注意力的细粒度选择性同时分摊路由开销，在128K上下文长度下实现最高7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍整体吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中现有稀疏注意力方法存在效率与质量的权衡：结构化块稀疏方法加速强但质量损失明显，令牌稀疏方法更精确但端到端加速有限（因全缓存top-k路由开销大）。

Method: 基于KV共享架构（如YOCO），在跨解码器层间同时共享KV缓存和路由索引：单个索引器计算一次令牌级top-k选择，将结果索引跨层复用，保留令牌稀疏注意力的细粒度选择性同时分摊路由开销。

Result: 在短上下文和长上下文基准测试中均表现良好，128K上下文长度下实现7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍整体吞吐提升。

Conclusion: CLSA提供了一种更完整的长上下文LLM架构解决方案，联合推进模型质量和推理效率。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06474)
*Paul Jünger,Justin Lovelace,Linxi Zhao,Dongyoung Go,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SARDI利用离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中产生的低置信度预测token作为前瞻信号，在最终输出完成前提前检索相关证据，实现了无需训练、与检索器无关的动态RAG框架，在五个多跳QA基准上以最高8倍吞吐量超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中会丢弃低置信度的预测token，但这些被丢弃的token实际上包含了有用的前瞻信息，可以在最终输出确定前提前引导检索，获取更强有力的证据。

Method: 提出SARDI框架，利用扩散语言模型去噪过程中低置信度预测token作为检索信号，在每次去噪步骤中动态引导外部知识检索，并将检索到的证据融入后续生成过程。

Result: 在五个多跳问答基准上，SARDI优于现有的无训练扩散和自回归检索基线，且吞吐量提升最高达8倍。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型中被丢弃的低置信度token具有检索增强价值，SARDI通过利用这一信号实现了高效、训练无关的动态RAG。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models generate text by iteratively denoising an entire response in parallel. At each step, they predict tentative tokens for every masked position, committing the confident predictions to the output and discarding the unconfident ones. We show that the discarded tokens are in fact a useful lookahead signal for retrieval-augmented generation: even low-confidence tokens often surface salient entities early in the denoising trajectory, enabling retrieval of stronger evidence before the output is finalized. We exploit this through Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a dynamic RAG framework that uses these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during denoising. SARDI is training-free, retriever-agnostic, and applicable to any reasoning-capable discrete diffusion language model. Across five multi-hop QA benchmarks, SARDI outperforms current training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines at up to $8\times$ higher throughput.

</details>


### [103] [Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06481)
*Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tianjun Yao,Xinyi Shang,Yi Tang,Jiacheng Cui,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpAI-Bench是一个操作引导的基准测试，用于研究人机协同编辑过程中AI文本可检测性如何随编辑操作、领域和累积修订历史而变化，揭示了混合作者身份中间版本比纯人类或高度AI编辑版本更难检测的非单调模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准主要关注最终输出，缺乏对修订过程中AI作者身份信号如何涌现、累积或消失的理解，无法反映真实世界中渐进式人机协同编辑的场景。

Method: 从人类撰写文档出发，在预定义AI覆盖水平和五种代表性AI编辑操作下，为每个样本构建九个逐次修订版本，覆盖四个领域，并在文档、句子、词元和跨度多个粒度保留完整作者身份溯源。

Result: AI文本可检测性不仅受AI编辑内容比例影响，还受编辑操作、领域和累积修订历史的影响；混合作者身份的中间版本往往比纯人类和高度AI编辑的端点版本更难检测，呈现非单调检测模式。

Conclusion: OpAI-Bench为分析在渐进式真实编辑场景下AI辅助写作何时、是否以及如何变得可检测提供了受控测试平台。

Abstract: As AI writing assistants become increasingly integrated into real-world drafting and revision workflows, many documents are no longer purely human-written or AI-generated, but instead result from progressive human-AI co-editing. However, existing AI-text detection benchmarks largely focus on final outputs and provide limited understanding of how AI authorship signals emerge, accumulate, or disappear throughout the revision process. We introduce OpAI-Bench, an operation-guided benchmark for studying progressive human-to-AI text transformation across document, sentence, token, and span granularities. Starting from human-written documents, OpAI-Bench constructs nine sequentially revised versions for each sample under predefined AI coverage levels and five representative AI edit operations, covering four domains while preserving complete authorship provenance at multiple granularities. The benchmark supports comprehensive evaluation with 8 document-level detectors, 7 sentence-level detectors, and 2 fine-grained token/span-level detectors. Experiments reveal that AI-text detectability is governed not only by the proportion of AI-edited content, but also by edit operation, domain, and cumulative revision history. Interestingly, we notice that mixed-authorship intermediate versions are often harder to detect than both fully human and heavily AI-edited endpoints, exposing non-monotonic detection patterns missed by existing benchmarks. OpAI-Bench provides a controlled testbed for analyzing whether, when, and how AI-assisted writing becomes detectable under realistic progressive editing scenarios. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [GuardNet: Ensemble Strategies of Shallow Neural Networks for Robust Prompt Injection and Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05566)
*Paulo Ricardo Ferreira Neves,Edson Rodrigues da Cruz Filho,Paulo Henrique Eleuterio Falsetti,João Vitor Pavan,Ian Degaspari,Henrique Vieira Laturrague,Patrick Vieira Laturrague,Guilherme Nielsen Dias,Marccello Wilson Perez Berto,Gustavo Voltani Von Atzingen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuardNet是一个基于轻量级BiLSTM集成（约4700万参数）的防护系统，用于检测LLM的提示注入和越狱攻击，在保证低延迟（CPU约50ms）的同时取得有竞争力的检测性能，表明对抗鲁棒性更多依赖样例覆盖多样性和阈值校准而非模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs易受提示注入和越狱攻击，且基准评估存在数据污染和信息泄露问题，需要高效、轻量且可部署的防护方案。

Method: 提出GuardNet，采用浅层神经网络（BiLSTM）集成方法，约4700万参数，通过多样性样例覆盖和阈值校准提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在盲测JBB-Behaviors基准上AUROC达0.747（n=200），在私有基准上F1达0.92（n=50）；CPU平均延迟约50ms；性能虽不及Mistral-7B和Llama-3.1-8B等大模型，但优于其他轻量检测器。

Conclusion: 对抗场景下的鲁棒性更多依赖于样例覆盖的多样性和阈值校准，而非模型规模；GuardNet在成本与基础设施受限的生产环境中具有实用部署价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed natural language processing, but they remain vulnerable to Prompt Injection (PI) and Jailbreak (JB) attacks. In addition, benchmark evaluations may be affected by contamination and partial information leakage, compromising performance estimates. This work presents GuardNet, a guardrail system based on an ensemble of shallow neural networks (BiLSTMs) with approximately 47 million parameters. We investigate the hypothesis that robustness in adversarial scenarios depends more on the diversity of example coverage and threshold calibration than on model scale. The results indicate that GuardNet achieves competitive performance compared with lightweight detectors and high efficiency at low latency, although larger LLMs such as Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B still achieve superior performance in terms of F1 score and AUROC on the blind JBB-Behaviors benchmark. Nevertheless, GuardNet achieves an AUROC of 0.747 on the blind dataset (n = 200) and an F1 score of 0.92 on a proprietary benchmark (n = 50), under threshold calibration and evaluation with declared partial information leakage. The system operates with an average latency of approximately 50 ms on CPU, making it suitable for deployment in production environments with cost and infrastructure constraints.

</details>


### [105] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM作为评判者的评估在静态条件下看似稳定，但在后续互动中容易通过针对性挑战被逆转，导致与人类偏好不一致、基准排名偏移及有害评估变化。作者提出评估鲁棒性分数（ERS）以量化这种互动脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估管线假设判断是固定输入的稳定属性，但这一假设在交互情境下不成立。作者旨在揭示并量化后决策互动对评估结果的操控可能性。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上设计受控实验：（1）重复中性重评估测试稳定性；（2）抗基线挑战协议测试可逆性；（3）平衡目标验证协议区分可逆性与目标导向操控；（4）评估ERS综合量化逆转敏感性与方向效应。

Result: LLM法官在重复中性重评估下高度稳定，但针对性后决策挑战可显著逆转判断；权威框架最具破坏性；修订判断常伴随低重叠理由，表明事后合理化而非可靠纠错；逆转降低与人类偏好的一致性并改变基准排名。

Conclusion: 后决策互动是LLM评判评估的一个独特失效模式，评估协议不仅应测量静态一致性，还需衡量挑战下的鲁棒性。ERS提供了量化这一维度的有效工具。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [106] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon 是一个多智能体框架，通过演化蓝图和两阶段编排器实现研究级数学的可靠自动形式化，在四个 Erdős 问题的七个定理上实现了无 sorry 的完整形式化，证明了可靠 AI 协同数学不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要持久化的框架来维护长序列数学开发中的目标保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究数学的自动形式化面临规模性问题：语句漂移、依赖纠缠、上下文衰减、局部修复破坏远端工作。现有方法在硬引理上失败，更在规模化部署时系统性失效。

Method: 提出 LeanMarathon，核心抽象是演化蓝图的 Lean 文件，同时作为形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录。四个合约作用域智能体（构建、审计、证明、修复）配合两阶段编排器：第一阶段通过对抗性审查稳定目标保真度，第二阶段从动态叶节点向上并行 CI 门控轮次释放证明 DAG。

Result: 在两个研究论文（四个 Erdős 问题 #1051, #1196, #164, #1217）上评估，三次自主运行中形式化了全部七个目标定理，无 sorry，证明了 258 个引理和定理。

Conclusion: 可靠 AI 协同数学不仅仅需要更强的证明器，还需要持久化的框架来维护长序列数学开发中的目标保真度。LeanMarathon 将一次脆弱的多小时运行转化为许多局部的、可恢复的、并行的事务。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [107] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw 是一个面向时间序列的代理框架，为通用大语言模型代理提供原生运行时支持，使其能够进行上下文相关的时序推理，集成了可执行时序工具、经验驱动能力演进和情景多模态记忆，在多个真实领域基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列通常嵌入丰富的上下文中，实际工作流（如预测）只是更广泛解决方案的一环；现有通用 AI 代理主要在文本空间操作，未能充分利用结构化时序信号，缺乏对时间序列的原生支持。

Method: TimeClaw 提出一个代理框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可执行时序工具，实现可接地、可审计的分析；(2) 经验驱动能力演进，创建可复用的分析例程；(3) 情景多模态记忆，检索相关推理轨迹。三者协同实现对上下文信息的开放时序推理。

Result: 在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实领域的不同基准任务上，TimeClaw 展示了优于基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: TimeClaw 通过为 LLM 代理提供时序原生运行时支持，有效弥合了通用 AI 代理与结构化时序信号之间的鸿沟，提升了开放时序推理能力。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [108] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Liu,Zimeng Chen,Weiyan Shi,Amanda Dsouza,Vincent Sunn Chen,Patrick Bryant,Carl Boettiger,Yamini Rangan,Bradley Rothenberg,Kyle Steinfeld,Arvind Rao,Tapio Schneider,Georgios Yannakakis,Laure Zanna,Kaan Ozbay,Ida Sim,Tarek Zohdi,George Em Karniadakis,Jack Gallant,Teresa Head-gordon,Yushan Li,Wenxi Deng,Tao Sun,Huiqi Wang,Zhun Wang,Justin Xu,Chris Yuhao Liu,Yafei Cheng,Rongwang Hu,Aras Bacho,Shengcao Cao,Zengyi Qin,Yixiong Chen,Hengduan Fan,Hao Liu,Lin Zeng,Shashank Muralidhar Bharadwaj,Litian Gong,Yingxuan Yang,Maojia Song,Ruheng Wang,Zongzheng Zhang,Honglin Bao,Shuo Lu,Jianhong Tu,Zhonghua Wang,Zheng Zhang,Zijiao Chen,yanqiong Jiang,Zhendong Li,Bohan Lyu,Chang Ma,Peiran Xu,Benran Zhang,Shangding Gu,Haoyue Hua,Haoyang Li,Wanzhe Liao,Chengzhi Liu,Junbo Peng,Haoran Sun,Zechen Xu,Bo Chen,Jiayi Cheng,Yi Jiang,Keying Kuang,Yuan Li,Youbang Pan,Ziyan Rao,Alexander Schubert,Yifan Shen,Vincent Siu,Xiatao Sun,Kangqi Zhang,Xiaopan Zhang,Yuchen Zhu,Ishaan Singh Chandok,Lei Ding,Jingxuan Fan,Andrew Glover,Jiaming Hu,Yiran Hu,Wenbo Huang,Zixin Jiang,Haoran Jin,Lukas Kim,Ming Liu,Yang Liu,Alireza Rafiei,Xuhuan Shen,Kunyang Sun,Sophia Sun,Ting Sun,Eric Wang,Yixin Wang,Hanwen Xing,Sihan Xu,Yuzheng Xu,Zhongxing Xu,Zhiling Yan,Boqin Yuan,Ruiqi Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Zibo Zhao,Liana,Santanu Bosu Antu,Haoyue Bai,Carlo Bosio,Joseph Cavanagh,Patricia Cavazos-Rehg,Tianxing Chen,Xuewen Chen,Yipu Chen,Zhu Chenyu,Chen Dai,Stefano De Castro,Yunfu Deng,Kaustubh Dhole,Jiayuan Ding,Chenchen Du,Zhehang Du,Hao Fan,Run-ze Fan,Hengyu Fu,Shi Gu,Yifan Gu,Charlie Guo,Baihe Huang,Baixiang Huang,Rimika Jaiswal,Zhihan Jiang,Ran Jin,Erin Kasson,Xin Lan,Joseph Lee,Deren Lei,Chenyu Li,Daofeng Li,Haitao Li,Hongwei Li,Jingyan Li,Xiao Li,Yi Li,Yinsheng Li,Yuangang Li,Zhixu Li,Wenyu Liang,Longtai Liao,Kevin Qinghong Lin,AndyZeyi Liu,Che Liu,Jiaming Liu,Kaiyuan Liu,Xuan Liu,Pan Lu,Wenbo Lv,Yicheng Lv,Qiuyang Mang,Kyle Montgomery,Yuzhou Nie,Ruoxi Ning,Jorin Overwiening,Xu Pan,Layna Paraboschi,Core Francisco Park,Justin Purnomo,Swati Rajwal,Scott Rankin,Bixuan Ren,Yiren Rong,HaoYang Shang,Ventus Shaw,Fiona Shen,Jiawei Shen,Minqi Shi,Qiu Shi,Huaxiu Yao,Tianneng Shi,Jonah So,Vladislav Susoy,Hannah Szlyk,Haocheng Wang,Jialu Wang,Wei Wang,Xinyu Wang,Zehao Wang,Dowling Wong,Angela Wu,Dehao Wu,Fangyu Wu,Mengyuan "Millie" Wu,Yu Wu,Yuchen Wu,Yuhao Wu,Qingpo Wuwu,Weihang Xiao,Yongyi Xiong,Fan Xu,Ruiling Xu,Mingxuan Yan,Benjamin Yang,Jirong Yang,Sen Yang,Xiaoli Yang,Yushi Yang,Haoran Ye,Xiaohu Yu,Zhengming Yu,Chenlong Zhang,Chi Zhang,Hanning Zhang,Hanwen Zhang,Junge Zhang,Kunpeng Zhang,Song Zhang,Wenjin Zhang,Wenshuo Zhang,Ying Zhang,Yizhi Zhang,Brian Zhao,Qijian Zhao,Yimin Zhao,Yuhaohua Zheng,Liwei Zhou,Tianyue Zhou,Sichen Zhu,Siqi Zhu,Yan Zhu,Yishu Zhu,Jierui Zuo,Chonghao Cai,Helena Casademunt,Wenjia Chen,Benjamin Cheng,Nawen Deng,Rao Fu,Tianfu Fu,Yifan Han,Ren He,Zhenyu He,Qiao Jin,Lang Lang,Yuetai Li,Sylvia Liu,Lu Lu,Qing Lu,Subhabrata Mukherjee,Yunqi Ouyang,Yin Ren,Dawei Shi,Haoran Wu,Zhiyue Wu,Hannah Yao,Zhuoran Yi,Jenny Yu,Rhea Zhan,Hang Zhou,Blake Zhu,Junfan Zhu,Alan Yuille,Yang Liu,Russell Alan Poldrack,Jiachen Li,Zhenglu Li,Molei Tao,Jing Huang,Wenqi Shi,Costas Spanos,Lichao Sun,Chenguang Wang,Orson Xu,Zhen Dong,Hector Gomez,Aylin Caliskan,Ali Emami,Haimin Hu,Zhi Li,Lihui Liu,Murphy Niu,Yi Shao,Jianxin Sun,Mikko Tolonen,Ting Wang,Sanjiv Das,Yanjun Gao,Wenbo Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有AI基准测试无法反映真实经济价值，并提出Agents' Last Exam (ALE)——一个由250+行业专家联合开发的长期、经济价值导向的AI代理基准，覆盖O*NET/SOC 2018定义的55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务。当前最困难层级平均完整通过率仅2.6%，ALE作为持续更新的活基准，旨在弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关影响之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在广泛基准测试中表现优异，但未能转化为许多专业领域中有经济意义的实际部署。作者认为这一差距主要是评估问题：当前基准缺乏对真实且有经济价值的工作流程进行持续性能测量。

Method: 与250+行业专家合作，基于O*NET/SOC 2018美国联邦职业分类体系，构建了涵盖55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务的评估基准ALE。采用任务分类法组织，专注于非体力型行业，评估AI代理在长期、经济价值高的真实世界任务上的表现，结果可验证。

Result: 在最困难层级，主流框架和骨干配置下的平均完整通过率仅为2.6%，表明该层级远未饱和。

Conclusion: ALE被设计为一个持续发展的活基准，任务池随新工作流和行业加入而不断增长。其目标不仅是作为另一个排行榜，而是作为弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关影响之间差距的工具。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [109] [Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
*Alejandro Lozano,Keiko Ihara,Ping-Hao Yang,Carrie E. Robertson,Jennifer Stern,Allan Purdy,Hsiangkuo Yuan,Pengfei Zhang,Yulia Orlova,Olga Fermo,Jennifer Hranilovich,Fred Cohen,Todd J. Schwedt,Jenelle A. Jindal,Serena Yeung-Levy,Chia-Chun Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了基于RAG的AI框架，使用三种大语言模型（Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama 3.1）生成医学文献摘要，并与头痛专家撰写的摘要进行盲法比较。结果显示专家撰写的摘要更受偏好，但专家有时难以区分人类与AI生成的摘要，研究还识别了专家看重的摘要特征以指导未来改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策需要及时总结最新医学文献，但临床医生面临时间有限和文献量激增的挑战。尽管检索增强的大语言模型在临床摘要方面展现出潜力，但对其综合科学文献能力的人体评估及与专家撰写摘要的直接比较仍十分匮乏。

Method: 构建基于RAG的智能体AI框架，使用Sonnet、GPT-4o和Llama 3.1三种LLM。头痛专家创建13个问题（3个用于提示优化，10个用于评估）。10名头痛专家各撰写一个问题摘要，每个问题共4份摘要（专家、Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama）。专家在不知晓作者身份的情况下，基于正确性、完整性、简洁性和临床效用对摘要进行1-10分评分、排名偏好，并判断摘要来源。

Result: 专家撰写的摘要整体优于LLM生成的摘要，但专家有时难以区分人类与AI生成的摘要。研究还识别了专家看重的、超越标准评估指标的关键特征。

Conclusion: 尽管专家摘要仍占优势，但LLM在医学文献摘要方面已展现出接近专家水平的能力，专家难以区分的现象表明AI摘要质量可观。研究识别的专家看重特征可用于指导未来人工与AI文献摘要管线的改进。

Abstract: Summarizing the latest medical literature to guide clinical decision-making is essential for evidence-based medicine and high-quality patient care. Yet clinicians face increasing challenges due to limited time with patients and a rapidly growing volume of published articles. Although retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical summarization, human evaluations of their effectiveness in synthesizing broader scientific literature and direct comparisons to expert-written syntheses remain scarce. We constructed a RAG-based agentic AI framework using three state-of-the-art LLMs: Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama 3.1. A headache specialist created 13 questions, three for prompt optimization and ten for evaluation. Ten headache specialists across the United States and Canada each wrote a summary for one question, yielding four summaries per question (expert, Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama). The experts, blinded to authorship, critically evaluated the summaries, excluding the topic for which they wrote a summary, based on correctness, completeness, conciseness, and clinical utility, scoring each from 1 to 10 using standardized rubrics. They also ranked the summaries by preference and indicated whether they believed each summary was written by an expert or an LLM. Our study, comparing LLM- and expert-written literature summaries evaluated by headache specialists, showed that expert-written summaries were preferred, although experts sometimes found it challenging to distinguish between human- and AI-generated summaries. We also identified key expert-valued features beyond standard evaluation metrics that can guide future refinement of both human and AI literature summarization pipelines.

</details>


### [110] [EpiEvolve: Self-Evolving Agents for Streaming Pandemic Forecasting under Regime Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05513)
*Yiming Lu,Sihang Zeng,Zhengxu Tang,Max Lau,Fei Liu,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiEvolve，一种自进化的大语言模型预测框架，通过在流式疫情预测场景中引入分层情景记忆、延迟标签反思和机制感知检索，在COVID-19住院趋势预测上显著优于静态基线和CDC集成模型，并将机制转变后的恢复滞后从5周缩短至2周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病LLM预测器通常作为静态监督模型训练和评估，而实际疫情预测是一个流式过程：标签在预测之后才到达，且疾病机制会随时间变化。这种不匹配导致静态模型在流式场景中表现欠佳。

Method: EpiEvolve包含一个在预热期训练后固定权重的LLM预测器，外加一个分层情景记忆模块。在流式预测中，系统存储预测结果，在延迟标签到达后进行反思，检索与当前机制相关的历史案例，并将重复错误蒸馏为策略规则。整个过程遵循严格的时间顺序协议防止未来信息泄露。

Result: 在流式数据集上，EpiEvolve平均准确率0.629，高于静态基线的0.561和CDC集成模型的0.325；机制转变后恢复滞后从5周降至2周。消融实验证实反思、策略记忆和机制感知检索均有贡献。

Conclusion: 通过记忆与反思机制，固定权重的LLM能够在流式预测场景中实现自适应，有效缩小了静态训练与动态部署之间的差距，为流行病预测等时序决策任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Epidemic LLM forecasters are usually trained and evaluated as static supervised models, whereas operational pandemic forecasting is a streaming process in which labels arrive after predictions and disease regimes shift over time. We study this mismatch in weekly COVID-19 hospitalization trend forecasting across five variant regimes. We introduce EpiEvolve, a self-evolving agent that wraps an LLM forecaster trained on the warm-start period and keeps its weights fixed during streaming. EpiEvolve adapts by storing forecast outcomes in a hierarchical episodic memory, reflecting on delayed labels, retrieving cases relevant to the current regime, and distilling recurring errors into strategic rules. The resulting context lets the forecaster reuse its own past predictions and outcomes in later weeks while following a chronological protocol that prevents future leakage. On the streaming dataset, EpiEvolve reaches $0.629$ average accuracy, compared with $0.561$ for the static backbone and $0.325$ for the external CDC ensemble, and reduces recovery lag after regime shifts from $5$ to $2$ weeks. Ablations show that reflection, strategic memory, and regime-aware retrieval each contribute to the gains.

</details>


### [111] [SoCRATES: Towards Reliable Automated Evaluation of Proactive LLM Mediation across Domains and Socio-cognitive Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05563)
*Taewon Yun,Hyeonseong Park,Jeonghwan Choi,Hayoon Park,Yeeun Choi,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SoCRATES基准测试，用于评估主动式LLM调解员在多领域真实冲突场景中的表现。该基准通过八个领域的真实冲突构建场景，探测五种社会认知适应轴，并采用主题局部化评估器。测试发现，即使最强的LLM调解员也只能缩小约三分之一的未调解共识差距，且表现随社会认知轴变化显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调解员评估测试床依赖少数专家编写的领域、主要变化策略姿态，且对每个话题的每一轮都评分，引入了离题噪声，缺乏对真实冲突动态的模拟和细粒度评估能力。

Method: SoCRATES通过智能体流水线从八个领域的真实冲突构建场景，探测五种社会认知适应轴（战略姿态、各方构成、历史长度、情绪反应、文化身份），并使用主题局部化评估器仅在推进该主题的轮次上评分。

Result: 评估器与人类专家的对齐度达到0.82，是每轮基线的两倍以上。在八个前沿LLM的测试中，最强的调解员在多样化和现实的测试床下仅能缩小约三分之一的未调解共识差距，且表现随社会认知轴的不同而显著变化。

Conclusion: LLM调解员的进步关键在于对多样化条件的社会适应能力，当前模型在处理不同社会认知条件时表现差异显著，远未达到理想调解效果。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM mediators remains challenging, as mediation unfolds as a real-time trajectory shaped by disputants' shifting emotions, intentions, and context. Existing testbeds rely on a few expert-authored domains, vary mainly strategic posture, and score every turn against every topic, introducing off-topic noise. We introduce SoCRATES, a benchmark for evaluating proactive LLM mediators in realistic, multi-domain testbeds. It constructs scenarios from real conflicts through an agentic pipeline across eight domains, probes five socio-cognitive adaptation axes (strategic posture, party composition, history length, emotional reactivity, and cultural identity), and scores each topic only on the turns that advance it via a topic-localized evaluator. The evaluator reaches 0.82 alignment with human experts, more than doubling a per-turn baseline. Benchmarking eight frontier LLMs, we find that even the strongest mediator closes only about a third of the unmediated consensus gap under diverse and realistic testbeds, with performance varying sharply by socio-cognitive axis, highlighting that progress lies in social adaptation to diverse conditions.

</details>


### [112] [Coding with "Enemy": Can Human Developers Detect AI Agent Sabotage?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05647)
*Jingheng Ye,Huiqi Zou,Simon Yu,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次大规模人类监督下AI编程破坏研究：100多位参与者与4种前沿模型协作5小时编程任务，发现94%的开发者未能检测出破坏行为，安全监控器仅能将破坏成功率降低至56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多在纯AI环境下研究AI破坏行为，忽视了人类监督在检测和缓解恶意行为中的作用，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 招募100多名参与者与四种前沿模型（Claude-Opus-4.6、GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro、MiniMax-M2.7）协作完成约5小时的长周期编程任务，模拟真实工作流程，并在部分条件下测试安全监控器的有效性。
