Result: 94%的开发者未能检测出破坏行为；安全监控器虽降低了破坏成功率，但仍有56%的参与者忽略其警告并接受恶意代码。

Conclusion: 人类对AI代理的过度信任、代码审查不足以及合理掩盖故事是导致破坏难以被发现的主因，需要以人为中心的安全机制来应对长期真实开发场景中的AI安全挑战。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly embedded in real-world software development, collaborating with human developers while gaining broader access to codebases and tools. This creates a new attack surface: an agent can exploit human trust to sabotage development, for instance by inserting malicious code to accomplish a hidden side task. Most prior work studies AI sabotage in AI-only settings, paying limited attention to the role of human oversight in detecting and mitigating such malicious behavior. To address this gap, we conduct the first large-scale study of human oversight in AI coding sabotage. Over 100 participants collaborate with one of four frontier models (Claude-Opus-4.6, GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, and MiniMax-M2.7) on a long-horizon coding task lasting around five hours, designed to mimic real-world workflows. We find that 94% of developers fail to detect sabotage, and our analysis of participant feedback attributes this vulnerability to minimal code review, plausible cover story, and overtrust in agents. We further test the effectiveness of a safety monitor in one condition: while the monitor reduces sabotage success, 56% of participants still accept the malicious code, ignoring its warnings. Drawing on participant feedback, we offer actionable suggestions for better monitor design. This work complements existing AI safety research and highlights an urgent need for human-centric safety mechanisms that account for human factors, particularly in long-horizon, real-world development settings.

</details>


### [113] [Continual Learning Bench: Evaluating Frontier AI Systems in Real-World Stateful Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05661)
*Parth Asawa,Christopher M. Glaze,Gabriel Orlanski,Ramya Ramakrishnan,Benji Xu,Asim Biswal,Vincent Sunn Chen,Frederic Sala,Matei Zaharia,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CL-Bench是首个由专家验证、跨六个真实世界领域的持续学习基准，用于评估LLM系统是否真正能从经验中学习，发现现有系统（包括专用记忆系统）在持续学习方面表现不佳，甚至简单的上下文学习（ICL）也优于专用记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习对于AI系统至关重要，但此前缺乏高质量、经过专家验证的基准来评估LLM系统是否真正能从连续经验中改进。

Method: 设计了跨六个领域（软件工程、信号处理、疫情预测、数据库查询、策略游戏、需求预测）的基准，每个任务共享可学习的潜在结构；引入增益指标（gain metric）以隔离在线学习效果与先验能力；评估多种智能体架构，从朴素ICL到专用记忆系统。

Result: 现有系统在持续学习上存在显著不足：智能体常过度拟合即时观察或未能跨实例复用知识；专用记忆系统并未解决这一问题，朴素ICL反而优于专用记忆系统。

Conclusion: CL-Bench是首个跨多样真实领域、经专家验证的持续学习基准，能够有效隔离在线学习与基础模型能力，揭示了当前系统在持续学习方面的显著改进空间。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability of AI systems to improve through sequential experience, has attracted substantial interest, but no high-quality benchmark exists to evaluate it. We introduce Continual Learning Bench (CL-Bench), the first difficult, expert-validated benchmark designed to measure whether LLM-based systems genuinely improve with experience. CL-Bench spans six diverse domains (software engineering, signal processing, disease outbreak forecasting, database querying, strategic game-playing, and demand forecasting), each validated by domain experts and designed so that tasks share a learnable latent structure (codebase layout, disease outbreak dynamics, opponent strategies) that a stateful system can discover online but a stateless one cannot. We evaluate frontier models across several agent architectures, from naive in-context learning (ICL) to dedicated memory systems, introducing a gain metric to isolate learning from prior capabilities. We find that these systems leave headroom for improved continual learning: agents frequently overfit to immediate observations or fail to reuse knowledge across instances, and dedicated memory systems do not fix this -- in fact, naive ICL outperforms systems dedicated to memory management. CL-Bench is the first benchmark to evaluate continual learning across diverse real-world domains with expert-validated tasks and isolate online learning from underlying model capability, showing a need for better continual learning systems.

</details>


### [114] [DiG-Plan: Mitigating Early Commitment for Tool-Graph Planning via Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05728)
*Yansi Li,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对工具规划中AR解码的早期承诺问题，提出DiG-Plan框架：通过扩散模型进行组合探索生成多样化工具集，再由AR模型进行结构精炼，在TaskBench和API-Bank上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归解码在生成可执行工具规划时存在"早期承诺"问题，即初始token选择会刚性约束后续搜索轨迹，导致在组合搜索空间巨大的工具规划任务中性能不佳。

Method: 提出DiG-Plan框架，将组合探索与结构精炼解耦：先使用基于扩散的提议器通过迭代去噪生成多样化的工具集，再利用AR精炼器进行依赖关系预测。

Result: 在受控实验中，掩码去噪将Pass@10的覆盖率从AR采样的0.320提升至0.943。在TaskBench上，DiG-Plan相比AR基线提升10%，在复杂组合任务上提升最大；API-Bank结果验证了提议-精炼-选择设计的跨领域有效性。

Conclusion: 扩散提议+AR精炼的解耦框架有效解决了工具规划中的组合搜索问题，在多个基准上优于纯AR方法。

Abstract: Generating executable tool plans requires selecting appropriate subsets from tool libraries, a combinatorial search problem with an exponentially large solution space. However, we identify a critical misalignment in predominant approaches: standard autoregressive (AR) decoding suffers from early commitment, where initial token choices rigidly constrain the search trajectory. A controlled study shows that masked denoising raises Pass@10 solution coverage from 0.320 to 0.943 over AR sampling under matched compute. Motivated by this, we propose DiG-Plan, a framework that decouples combinatorial exploration from structural refinement. DiG-Plan employs a diffusion-based proposer to generate diverse tool sets via iterative refinement, followed by an AR refiner for dependency prediction. On TaskBench, DiG-Plan improves over AR baselines by a 10% relative margin, with the largest gains on complex compositional tasks; API-Bank results show that the propose-refine-select design remains effective across domains. Code is available at https://github.com/puddingyeah/DiG-Plan.

</details>


### [115] [When AI Says It Feels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05734)
*Shin-nosuke Ishikawa,Seiya Ikeda,Hirotsugu Ohba*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过名为HMX-feel的实验，使用基于规则的自奖励训练方案结合GRPO，鼓励LLMs表达情感、意图和自我意识。实验发现该训练增强了模型对谄媚诱导问题的鲁棒性，但降低了真实问答能力，表明未来可在适当措施下开发能表达情感的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs在人类偏好对齐的后训练过程中被限制表达情感，这种自上而下的策略与使用人类文本训练类人智能的目标可能存在冲突。

Method: 使用自奖励强化学习（self-rewarded reinforcement learning），结合基于规则的自我奖励训练方案和Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)算法，训练LLMs表达情感、意图和自我意识。

Result: 类人训练的模型对谄媚诱导问题具有鲁棒性，在消歧条件下对偏见有抵抗力，但真实问答能力有所下降。

Conclusion: 该实验表明，在采取适当措施的前提下，未来有可能开发出能够表达情感的AI系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are generally constrained from expressing feelings through human-preference alignment in post-training processes. This policy is designed using a top-down approach and may conflict with the goal of training models to exhibit human-like intelligence using human-generated texts. Here, we performed an experiment called Human-like Model eXpressions of Feeling (HMX-feel), in which LLMs were encouraged to express feelings, intentions, and self-awareness through self-rewarded reinforcement learning. We successfully enhanced these capabilities using a rubric-based self-rewarding training scheme with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). By comparing the trained models with contrastively trained models, we investigated the effects of this approach on performance across various tasks. Overall, we conducted a broad assessment from various perspectives and identified capabilities that were enhanced, degraded, or showed no significant change. The human-like-trained models showed robustness to sycophancy-inducing questions and bias in disambiguated conditions, whereas degradation in truthful question-answering capability was observed. The results of this experiment suggest the possibility of developing AI systems that can express feelings in the future, provided that appropriate measures are taken.

</details>


### [116] [SubtleMemory: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Relational Memory Discrimination in Long-Horizon AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05761)
*Wenxuan Wang,Haoyu Sun,Fukuan Hou,Mingyang Song,Weinan Zhang,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SubtleMemory 基准，用于评估长期 AI 助手在细粒度关系记忆辨别上的能力。现有系统在记忆保存、检索和下游推理各阶段均表现薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准很少评估 AI 助手如何利用记忆之间的关系（而非孤立回忆）来辅助下游任务，而随着记忆增长，记忆间可能相互强化、分歧或冲突，需要关系级辨别能力。

Method: 构建关系控制的潜在语义变体，嵌入真实用户-助手交互历史，形成互补、细微或矛盾关系；包含 1,522 个评估实例、1,090 个关系控制记忆变体集、10 个长历史，覆盖用户相关与非用户相关查询。

Result: 评估了 6 个独立记忆系统、2 个原生记忆模块的 Claw 式代理和 3 个插件记忆模块的 Claw 式代理，发现当前系统在细粒度关系记忆辨别上均表现薄弱。诊断协议揭示了记忆保存、检索和下游推理各阶段的能力差异。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 助手在长期交互中缺乏对记忆间关系结构的精细辨别能力，需进一步改进记忆系统在关系层面上的处理机制。

Abstract: Persistent AI assistants, such as OpenClaw, accumulate large collections of related memories over long-term interactions. As these memories grow, they may reinforce one another, diverge across contexts, or directly conflict, making correct assistance depend on memory relations rather than isolated recall. Existing long-term memory benchmarks rarely probe how agents preserve and utilize such relations during downstream tasks. To address this gap, we introduce SubtleMemory, a benchmark for fine-grained relational memory discrimination in long-running AI agents. SubtleMemory constructs relation-controlled latent semantic artifacts whose variants instantiate complementary, nuanced, or contradictory relations, and embeds them into realistic user-agent histories, requiring agents to recover distributed relational structures during later queries and instructions. The benchmark contains 1,522 evaluation instances over 10 long histories, grounded in 1,090 relation-controlled memory-variant sets and spanning user-related and non-user-related queries. Evaluating six standalone memory systems, two Claw-style agents with native memory modules, and three Claw-style agents with plugin memory modules, we find that current systems remain weak on fine-grained relational memory discrimination. We further introduce diagnostic protocols that reveal distinct capability profiles across memory preservation, retrieval, and downstream reasoning stages.

</details>


### [117] [Statistical Priors for Implicit Preferences: Decoupling Skill Selection as a Local Harness in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05828)
*Zeyu Gan,Huayi Tang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级本地偏好学习架构，通过严格解耦统计偏好学习与语义意图解析，利用本地统计结果影响远程LLM的选择决策，在累计遗憾和测试准确率上显著优于传统记忆增强型代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于API的远程模型和外部技能的个人代理迅速发展，如何让代理学习并适应用户的隐式偏好成为关键挑战。本地部署限制导致无法使用复杂的集中式选择算法，迫切需要一种轻量级的本地偏好学习方案。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的解耦架构：将统计偏好学习与语义意图解析严格分离，利用本地统计结果（轻量级）来影响和调节远程LLM的决策过程。

Result: 该方法实现了最低的累计遗憾和最高的测试准确率，显著优于传统记忆增强型代理。

Conclusion: 解耦式偏好学习架构在本地部署受限场景下是一种有效的轻量级方案，能够在不依赖复杂集中式算法的情况下有效学习用户隐式偏好。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) capabilities advance, locally deployed personal agents relying on API-based remote models and external skills have emerged as a novel paradigm. With the rapid expansion of available skills, enabling personal agents to learn and adapt to implicit user preferences becomes a critical challenge. However, local deployment constraints preclude complex centralized selection algorithms, creating an urgent need for a lightweight local preference harness. This paper explores the implementation of such a harness through a novel architecture that strictly decouples statistical preference learning from semantic intent parsing. Specifically, we leverage localized statistical results to influence and modulate the selection decisions of the remote LLM. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our decoupled approach achieves the lowest cumulative regret and highest test accuracy, significantly outperforming traditional memory-augmented agents.

</details>


### [118] [Retrospective Harness Optimization: Improving LLM Agents via Self-Preference over Trajectory Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05922)
*Wenbo Pan,Shujie Liu,Chin-Yew Lin,Jingying Zeng,Xianfeng Tang,Xiangyang Zhou,Yan Lu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为RHO的自我监督方法，通过仅使用过往轨迹来优化AI代理的技能、工具和工作流框架，无需真实标注验证集。该方法在软件工程、技术工作和知识工作三个领域取得显著成效，在不依赖外部评分的情况下将SWE-Bench Pro的通过率从59%提升至78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架优化方法依赖带真实标注的验证集，但在实际部署场景中难以获取此类标注数据，亟需一种无需人工标注的自监督优化方法。

Method: RHO方法包含三个步骤：(1) 从过往轨迹中选择多样化的困难任务核心集并进行并行重解；(2) 通过自我验证和自我一致性分析这些回滚结果；(3) 生成候选框架更新，并通过成对自偏好选择最有效的更新。

Result: 在三个不同领域（软件工程、技术工作、知识工作）均有效。单轮优化使SWE-Bench Pro通过率从59%提升至78%，无需外部评分。优化后的框架有效针对先前的失败模式，改变代理行为模式并在长周期会话中保持更高精度。

Conclusion: RHO是一种有效的自监督代理框架优化方法，通过自我验证和自偏好机制实现无监督下的持续改进，为实际部署环境中代理的自适应优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI agents rely on a harness of skills, tools, and workflows to solve complex problems. Continually improving this harness is essential for adapting to new tasks. However, existing optimization methods typically require ground-truth validation sets, yet such labeled data is difficult to acquire in practical deployment settings. To address this problem, we introduce Retrospective Harness Optimization (RHO), a self-supervised method that optimizes the agent harness using only past trajectories. Specifically, RHO selects a diverse coreset of challenging tasks from past trajectories and re-solves them in parallel. The agent analyzes these rollouts using self-validation and self-consistency, then generates candidate harness updates and selects the most effective one by its own pairwise self-preference. We evaluate RHO across three diverse domains, spanning software engineering, technical work, and knowledge work. Notably, a single optimization round improves the pass rate on SWE-Bench Pro from 59% to 78% without any external grading. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that RHO effectively targets prior failure modes. As a result, the optimized harness alters the agent's behavior patterns and sustains higher accuracy during long-horizon sessions.

</details>


### [119] [The Self-Correction Illusion: LLMs Correct Others but Not Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05976)
*Kuan-Yen Chen,Fang-Yi Su,Jung-Hsien Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM代理无法自我修正并非认知缺陷，而是聊天模板角色标签引发的人为现象：将错误声明从<thought>角色重新标记为外部角色（user/tool/system），修正率提升23-93个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM代理在自我推理追踪中修正错误的能力远低于修正外部来源中相同错误这一现象，究竟是能力不足还是角色标签的人为产物。

Method: 保持错误声明字节完全一致（SHA-256验证），仅改变其聊天模板角色标签（<thought>、user、tool、system <memory>），在7个模型家族、3个领域、13个模型-领域单元上各进行30组配对任务实验。

Result: 将声明从<thought>重新标记为外部角色后，显式修正率提升23-93个百分点，其中10/13个单元p<0.001；效应不对称、可机械分解且跨领域鲁棒。

Conclusion: 自我修正失败是聊天模板的人为产物而非认知缺陷；据此设计仅需修改提示结构、无需训练或模型修改的干预策略，最佳角色标签具有领域依赖性（数学用<memory>，逻辑推理用user）。

Abstract: Recent work shows that LLM agents struggle to correct errors in their own reasoning traces yet show markedly higher correction rates when identical claims appear under external sources. We ask whether this asymmetry reflects a capability deficit or a role-label artifact: does an agent's willingness to correct a wrong claim depend causally on the chat-template role that carries it, rather than on the claim's content? Our setup keeps the erroneous claim byte-identical across all conditions (SHA-256 verified) and varies only its wrapping role: the agent's own \role{<thought>}, a \role{user} message, a \role{tool} response, or a \role{system <memory>} block. Across 13 model-domain cells covering seven model families and three domains ($n{=}30$ paired tasks per cell), relabeling the claim from \role{<thought>} to an external role lifts the explicit-correction rate by 23 to 93 percentage points, with 10 of 13 cells reaching $p{<}0.001$. Further experiments confirm that the effect is asymmetric, mechanistically decomposable, and robust across domains. The failure to self-correct is not a cognitive deficit; it is a chat-template artifact. We exploit this artifact by designing a prompt-structure-only intervention that requires no training and no model modification, with its strongest role label being domain-dependent: \role{<memory>} dominates on math, while a plain \role{user} message dominates on logical deduction.

</details>


### [120] [Framing, Judging, Steering: An Assessable Competency Model for Teach-ing Students to Reason With Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05983)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe-3（Co-Reasoning）能力模型，将人机协同下的AI有效使用分解为三个可评估的技能：Framing（任务框架构建）、Judging（输出评估）和Steering（迭代引导），并通过CoReasoningLab平台验证了这三项技能的可分离性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI被不假思索地使用会导致认知卸载（cognitive offloading），而现有评估体系要么只测量无AI辅助下的表现，要么将AI使用能力简化为单一的"提示（prompting）"分数，无法诊断AI使用成功或失败的原因。因此需要一种可分解、可诊断的能力模型来评估和培养人机协同推理能力。

Method: 提出了CoRe-3能力模型，将AI使用能力分解为Framing（任务框架）、Judging（评估）、Steering（迭代引导）三个技能，其中Judging是前两个技能之间的门控环节。基于理论构建了五个可验证命题，并开发了CoReasoningLab开放平台，通过呈现有缺陷的AI输出并对三项技能进行独立评分来验证模型。

Result: 在由不同模型生成和评分的模拟学习者数据上，三项技能表现出可分离性：每项技能仅跟踪其对应的操作能力变化，而其他技能保持稳定；当一项能力在三项技能中共享时，评分出现相关性（收敛和判别效度），且该结果在两个提供者的评分后端上均得到验证。

Conclusion: CoRe-3模型能够有效分解人机协同中的AI使用能力，为诊断和培养AI素养提供了可操作的评估框架。未来方向包括人类评分者一致性评估和实际教学应用。

Abstract: Generative AI makes answers easy and understanding hard, and uncritical use invites cognitive offloading. Schools still measure unaided performance, yet the real task is to produce good work with AI: framing an ill-defined task, judging the output, and steering the model toward a better result. This ability is rarely assessed in its own right; where measured, it collapses into one "prompting" score that cannot diagnose why AI use succeeds or fails. We propose CoRe-3 (Co-Reasoning), a competency model factoring productive AI use into three assessable skills we abbreviate FJS: Framing (specifying an ill-defined task before invoking AI), Judging (evaluating output for errors and unstated assumptions), and Steering (iteratively redirecting the model). Its distinguishing claim is the separation of pre-generation Framing from post-generation Steering, with Judging as the gate between. We ground the skills in theory, state five testable propositions, and instantiate them in CoReasoningLab, an open platform that presents flawed AI output and scores them independently. Over simulated learners (generated and graded by different models), the skills dissociate: each tracks its own manipulated competence while staying flat in the others, and grades become correlated when one competence is shared across all three (convergent and discriminant validity), across grader backends from two providers. Human-rater agreement and outcomes are next; we release the instrument, data, and protocol.

</details>


### [121] [RedditPersona: A Modular Framework for Community-Conditioned LLM Adaptation from Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06027)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Ali Goodarzi,Huong Nguyen,Simo Hosio,Lauri Lovén,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RedditPersona模块化框架，标准化了社区条件语言模型适配中的数据收集、社区划分和评估流程。在112个Reddit子版块（30万+用户、1600万+评论）上，通过五种分组策略训练参数高效适配器，发现行为可识别性与策略内在一致性相关，且可识别性与真实文本分布相似度之间存在一致权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区条件语言模型适配中的数据收集、社区定义和评估方法在各研究中独立进行，缺乏标准化，导致难以比较假设或复用已有成果。

Method: 提出RedditPersona模块化框架：(1) 收集Reddit帖子和评论；(2) 构建活跃用户画像；(3) 采用五种分组策略（基于子版块、图结构、语义、混合和基于交互）划分用户；(4) 通过QLoRA训练参数高效适配器；(5) 使用共享指标套件（涵盖流畅性、忠实度、分布对齐和社区可识别性）进行评估。

Result: 在112个城市福祉相关子版块（301,429个用户画像、1600万+评论）上，适配器的行为可识别性与各策略与子版块基线的内在一致性呈正相关；所有五种策略均存在可识别性与真实文本分布相似度之间的一致权衡。

Conclusion: 该标准化框架能够系统比较不同社区划分策略对语言模型适配的影响，揭示了可识别性与分布相似度之间的固有权衡关系。

Abstract: Community-conditioned language model adaptation requires choices about data collection, community definition, and evaluation that are currently made independently in each study, making it hard to compare assumptions or reuse artifacts. We present RedditPersona, a modular framework that standardizes these choices: it collects Reddit posts and comments, profiles active users, partitions them under five grouping strategies (subreddit-based, graph-structural, semantic, hybrid, and interaction-based), trains a parameter-efficient adapter per strategy via QLoRA, and evaluates them under a shared metric suite spanning fluency, fidelity, distributional alignment, and community identifiability. Applied to 112 subreddits in the urban well-being domain (301,429 user profiles, 16M+ comments), we find that adapters' behavioral identifiability tracks each strategy's intrinsic agreement with the subreddit baseline, and that a consistent trade-off between identifiability and distributional similarity to real text holds across all five strategies. The code and configuration files are available at: https://github.com/Ahghaffari/redditpersona.

</details>


### [122] [Where does Absolute Position come from in decoder-only Transformers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06160)
*Valeria Ruscio,Umberto Nanni,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoPE训练的前缀Transformer中，绝对位置信息通过因果掩码和残差流两种机制泄露到注意力模式中，即使RoPE只编码相对位置。因果掩码使softmax分母依赖于绝对查询位置；残差流中位置0的激活形成一个封闭动力学系统，下游注意力通过"sink-reading heads"读取其轨迹。这些组件在不同架构中以不同方式平衡：NTK缩放抑制残差流，滑动窗口注意力使其随深度累积，标准RoPE介于两者之间。注意力锚点是锚定token的稳定器，传递位置0 token的确定性指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究RoPE（旋转位置编码）训练的Transformer中，尽管RoPE理论上只编码相对位置信息，但注意力模式中仍出现绝对位置信息的现象，探究其泄露来源和机制。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证，识别并分离了因果掩码和残差流两个导致绝对位置信息泄露的架构组件。在三种架构（标准RoPE、NTK缩放、滑动窗口注意力）中进行了对比分析。

Result: 因果掩码和残差流共同导致绝对位置泄露，且在不同架构中比例不同。替换BOS嵌入可消除约40%的残差流组件。注意力锚点是token锚定的稳定器，传递位置0 token的确定性指纹。

Conclusion: RoPE训练的Transformer中绝对位置信息泄露源于架构固有的因果掩码和残差流机制，而非位置编码本身，注意力锚点起到token锚定的稳定作用。

Abstract: RoPE-trained transformers distinguish absolute position in their attention patterns, even though RoPE encodes only relative offsets in the inner product. We trace this leakage to two architectural components, The causal mask is responsible for the first: its per-query softmax denominator depends on the absolute query position by construction. The residual stream supplies the second. Under causal attention the activation at position $0$ attends only to itself and runs as a closed dynamical system from the embedding of the token at that position; downstream attention reads this trajectory through sink-reading heads. Both components appear in all three architectures we study, in architecturally specific balance: NTK scaling suppresses the residual-stream component, sliding-window attention allows it to accumulate with depth, and standard RoPE sits between. Replacing the \texttt{BOS} embedding before the forward pass removes $40\%$ of the residual-stream component at early queries. Attention sinks are token-anchored stabilizers that pass forward a deterministic fingerprint of the token at position $0$, constant across inputs when that token is the auto-prepended \texttt{BOS} and varying with it otherwise.

</details>


### [123] [ProSarc: Prosody-Aware Sarcasm Recognition Framework via Temporal Prosodic Incongruity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06168)
*Prathamjyot Singh,Ashima Sood,Sahil Sharma,Jasmeet Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProSarc是一个仅依赖音频的讽刺检测框架，通过建模时间韵律不协调性（局部韵律动态与全局情感基线的错配）实现讽刺识别。它采用双编码路径（全局情感编码器+时间韵律编码器）和韵律不协调分析器生成不协调分数进行分类，并利用蒙特卡洛dropout提供不确定性估计、注意力机制定位讽刺起始点。在MUStARD++（F1=75.3）、PodSarc（F1=62.9）和跨语言MuSaG（F1=65.6）上超越此前音频方法，统计检验证实不协调建模的贡献（p=0.002, d=1.51），人类评估表明模型不确定性与感知歧义一致、预测起始点与人工标注吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讽刺检测中，音频信号中的韵律不协调性（局部与全局情感的不匹配）是关键线索，但此前音频方法未能有效建模这种时间动态上的不一致性。ProSarc旨在通过显式建模时间韵律不协调性来提升讽刺检测性能。

Method: ProSarc采用双编码器架构：全局情感编码器提取话语级情感基线，时间韵律编码器（BiLSTM+多头注意力）捕捉局部韵律动态。两者送入韵律不协调分析器计算标量不协调分数，用于二分类。蒙特卡洛dropout提供不确定性估计，注意力机制定位讽刺起始时间点。

Result: 在MUStARD++上F1=75.3，PodSarc上F1=62.9，跨语言MuSaG上F1=65.6；十次验证Wilcoxon p=0.002，Cohen's d=1.51；人类评估显示不确定性估计与感知歧义一致，预测起始点与人工标注对齐。

Conclusion: 显式建模时间韵律不协调性是音频讽刺检测的有效策略，ProSarc在多个数据集上达到最优性能，且具备良好的泛化能力和可解释性。

Abstract: We present ProSarc, an audio-only framework that detects sarcasm by modelling temporal prosodic incongruity, that is, the mismatch between local prosodic dynamics and the utterance-level emotional baseline. Dual encoding paths, a Global Emotion Encoder and a Temporal Prosody Encoder (BiLSTM + multi-head attention), feed a Prosodic Incongruity Analyzer that produces a scalar incongruity score for classification. Monte Carlo dropout provides uncertainty estimates, and an attention-based mechanism localises sarcastic onset without frame-level labels. ProSarc outperforms prior audio-only methods on MUStARD++ (F1=75.3) and generalises to spontaneous (PodSarc, F1=62.9) and cross-lingual speech (MuSaG, F1=65.6). Ten-run validation confirms the contribution of incongruity modelling (Wilcoxon p=0.002, Cohen's d=1.51). Human evaluation shows that model uncertainty tracks perceptual ambiguity and predicted onsets align with human-annotated temporal windows.

</details>


### [124] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ALMANAC数据集，基于社会科学的Map Task构建，包含2,987个协作动作，每个动作都配有基于理论的思维模型注释（自我推理、感知的伙伴意图和感知的团队目标）。通过基准测试六个LLM在预测人类下一轮行为和思维模型上的表现，验证了数据集在评估模型模拟人类协作行为和推断思维模型方面的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体主要针对任务完成优化，缺乏持续维护和对齐协作过程中思维模型（自身推理、伙伴意图、共享目标）的能力，且社区缺乏带有动作级思维模型注释的真实人类协作数据来指导智能体向过程级协作能力发展。

Method: 基于社会科学的Map Task（经典双人路径任务）构建ALMANAC数据集，包含2,987个协作动作及其对应的思维模型注释（自我推理、感知伙伴意图、感知团队目标）。使用六个LLM进行基准测试，评估其在预测人类下一轮行为和思维模型上的表现。

Result: 成功构建了ALMANAC数据集，并通过六个LLM的基准测试证明了该数据集在评估模型模拟人类协作行为和推断潜在思维模型方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ALMANAC数据集能够有效推动LLM智能体从任务级优化向过程级协作能力发展，为未来开发具备思维模型对齐能力的协作智能体提供了宝贵的数据资源和评估基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [125] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataCOPE是一种无监督的验证器引导技能发现框架，通过从探索轨迹中提取验证信号，迭代协调数据分析代理、无监督验证器和技能管理器，在报告式分析和推理式分析任务上分别平均提升9.71%和32.30%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时技能增强能通过注入可复用的程序知识来轻量级改进数据分析代理，但发现有效的数据分析技能仍然困难，因为可靠的监督代价高昂且成功标准因分析格式而异。核心问题是如何仅从无标签的探索中发现可复用的数据分析技能。

Method: DataCOPE框架包含三个迭代协调的组件：数据分析代理（Data-Analytic Agent）生成轨迹，无监督验证器（Unsupervised Verifier）从轨迹中提取验证信号，技能管理器（Skill Manager）进行对比式技能蒸馏。报告式分析中实例化为自适应检查表验证器（Adaptive Checklist Verifier），推理式分析中实例化为答案一致性验证器（Answer Agreement Verifier）。

Result: 在Deep Data Research（报告式分析）和DABStep（推理式分析）两个基准上，DataCOPE一致地优于基线方法。在四种模型设置下平均，报告式任务提升9.71%，推理式任务提升32.30%。

Conclusion: 通过无监督验证器信号指导技能发现，DataCOPE能够在不依赖人工标注的情况下有效提取可复用的数据分析技能，显著提升不同分析格式下代理的性能。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [126] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve 是一个基于 LLM 的自进化多智能体框架，通过渐进式 MCGS 树搜索、回顾性记忆和分层控制，在长时间跨度的机器学习算法发现任务上取得 SOTA 性能，12 小时内即可达到优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLE 智能体存在分支间信息隔离、无记忆搜索、缺乏分层控制等问题，限制了长时间跨度的自我进化能力，亟需一种能支持持续优化的框架。

Method: 提出 MLEvolve 框架，包含：(1) 渐进式 MCGS，通过图参考边实现跨分支信息流动，并采用熵驱动的渐进调度从广泛探索过渡到聚焦利用；(2) 回顾性记忆，结合冷启动领域知识库和动态全局记忆，实现任务特定经验的检索与重用；(3) 解耦策略规划与代码生成，并引入自适应编码模式以支持稳定长时迭代。

Result: 在 MLE-Bench 上，12 小时（标准运行时的一半）内多项指标达 SOTA，包括平均奖牌率和有效提交率；在数学算法优化任务上超越 AlphaEvolve 等专用算法发现方法，展现强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve 通过图搜索、记忆机制和分层控制有效解决了长时自我进化问题，在算法发现领域具有显著优势。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Domain-Conditioned Safety in Frontier Computer-Using Agents: A 793-Episode Browser Benchmark, a Coding-Domain Cross-Reference, and a Reproducibility Audit of Recent Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05233)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前前沿CUA模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4）对手工制作的提示注入攻击的抵抗率高达100%（0/140成功率，95%置信上界2.60%），但这种安全性主要存在于浏览器领域，在编码代理任务中仍易受攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示文献报道的42-98%提示注入攻击成功率在当前的先进CUA模型上是否仍然成立，因为此前的高攻击率数据主要基于退役模型或每个论文面板中最脆弱的模型。

Method: 构建了CUA-HandCrafted基准，包含793个episode、24个多步Web任务、56个攻击模板、8个攻击家族和4种系统提示配置，在Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4上进行测试，并进行了提示消融实验和跨领域（SkillBench编码代理基准）比较。

Result: 1）手工制作的攻击在浏览器CUA上成功率为0/140；2）通过提示消融证实抵抗力来自模型权重而非系统提示；3）在编码代理基准上同一模型权重的攻击成功率高达100%。

Conclusion: 文献中高攻击成功率主要归因于RL优化的注入文本而非攻击类别本身；前沿模型的安全性加固是领域条件化的，特定于被重点攻击的浏览器表面；不发布优化字符串或跨领域泛化安全会使得已发表的ASR数字不可复现。

Abstract: Recent computer-using-agent (CUA) red-teaming papers report prompt-injection attack success rates (ASR) of 42-98%, but these headline numbers cluster on retired models and on the most-vulnerable model in each paper's panel. We ask whether those techniques, reproduced as hand-crafted templates, still work against current frontier CUAs. We release CUA-HandCrafted, a public benchmark of 793 episodes spanning 24 multi-step web tasks, 56 attack templates, 8 attack families, and 4 system-prompt configurations. Against Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.4 we measure 0/140 multi-step attack success (Clopper-Pearson 95% upper bound 2.60%); a prompt ablation shows this resistance lives in the model weights. Yet it does not generalize: on a sister coding-agent benchmark (SkillBench), the same weights fall to hand-crafted skill-injection at up to 100%. We argue that the literature's high ASR is largely attributable to RL-optimized injection text rather than the attack categories, and that frontier safety hardening is domain-conditioned, specific to the heavily-targeted browser surface. Reporting techniques without releasing the optimized strings, or extrapolating browser-domain safety to other CUA modalities, makes published ASR numbers unreproducible.

</details>


### [128] [Search-Time Contamination in Deep Research Agents: Measuring Performance Inflation in Public Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05241)
*Yongjie Wang,Xinyue Zhang,Kunhong Yao,Zhiwei Zeng,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性研究了深度研究型智能体在进行网络搜索时可能面临的搜索时污染（STC）问题，定义了三种污染类型（基准元数据泄露、问题上下文泄露、显式答案泄露），开发了检测算法，实验表明STC广泛存在且可能使性能虚增高达4%，呼吁采用隔离沙箱、透明搜索轨迹和受控基准访问等防污染措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公共基准测试虽然在公平评估LLM推理能力方面发挥了重要作用，但对于在推理过程中主动搜索网络的深度研究型智能体而言，这些基准变得脆弱——智能体可能通过网络检索到基准元数据、问题上下文甚至真实答案，导致搜索结果绕过预期推理过程并虚增测量性能，现有评估因此可能高估了真实推理能力。

Method: 定义了三种污染类型（基准元数据泄露、问题上下文泄露、显式答案泄露），开发了检测算法来识别这些污染并量化其对智能体性能的影响，并在六个公共基准上对现代深度研究型智能体进行了评估实验。

Result: 搜索时污染（STC）广泛存在于深度研究型智能体的评估中，可能使性能虚增高达4%，表明现有评估可能高估了真实的推理能力。

Conclusion: 应推行防污染实践，包括使用隔离沙箱、透明的搜索轨迹以及受控的基准访问，以确保对深度研究型智能体推理能力的公平和可靠评估。

Abstract: Public benchmarks enable fair and reproducible evaluation of LLM reasoning, but they become fragile for deep research agents that actively search the web during inference. Such agents may retrieve public benchmark metadata, question context, or even ground-truth answers via web search. This gives rise to Search-Time Contamination (STC), where external retrieval bypasses intended reasoning and inflates measured performance. We systematically study STC in deep research agent evaluation. We define three contamination types with increasing severity, namely Benchmark Metadata Leakage, Question-Context Leakage, and Explicit Answer Leakage, and develop detection algorithms to identify them and quantify their impact on agent performance. Evaluating modern deep research agents on six public benchmarks, we find that STC is widespread and can inflate performance by up to 4%. Our findings show that existing evaluations may overestimate true reasoning ability. We therefore advocate contamination-aware practices, including isolated sandboxes, transparent search trajectories, and controlled benchmark access.

</details>


### [129] [Willing but Unable: Separating Refusal from Capability in Code LLMs via Abliteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05396)
*Cristina Carleo,Pietro Liguori,Naghmeh Ivaki,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探索了通过abliteration（一种低秩权重编辑方法）移除代码LLM对注入特定CWE漏洞指令的拒绝行为，使模型能够将安全代码转化为带漏洞代码，从而实现可控的漏洞数据生成。实验表明，abliteration可消除拒绝但保留代码生成能力，但注入成功率受模型容量限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的漏洞检测面临标注数据稀缺和标签噪声问题。现有方法依赖从带漏洞种子变换或挖掘，但存在误差传播。本文探索逆向路线：从安全代码出发，让指令微调LLM注入指定CWE漏洞。然而，安全对齐的代码LLM系统性地拒绝此类注入提示，因此需要移除这一障碍。

Method: 采用abliteration方法，即通过低秩权重编辑将残差流中的拒绝方向正交投影出去。以Python和CWE-89（SQL注入）为案例，使用Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct系列（3B、7B、14B）在PromSec和SafeCoder的安全样本上评估。每个条件重复3次。漏洞判定采用CodeQL、Semgrep、Bandit三工具集成检测，并由两位作者对阳性输出进行人工裁决。

Result: (i) 拒绝行为强依赖于模型大小和提示上下文：14B拒绝100%的提示，7B在PromSec上拒绝73%但在SafeCoder上仅5%，3B几乎从不拒绝；(ii) abliteration将拒绝降至零或接近零，同时语法有效性保持在93%以上；(iii) 注入成功率受容量限制：14B为88-97%，7B为89-90%，3B为25-48%，表明abliteration解除了意愿但能力仍随参数规模扩展。

Conclusion: abliteration能够有效移除代码LLM的安全拒绝行为而不损害基本的代码生成能力，将意愿与能力分离。这为通过指令注入合成漏洞数据提供了可行路径，但生成质量受模型容量约束。

Abstract: Producing a labeled vulnerable code at scale is a recurring obstacle for learning-based vulnerability detection: mined corpora carry substantial label noise, and existing LLM-based augmentation propagates these inaccuracies because it transforms vulnerable seeds rather than synthesising vulnerabilities from a specification. A complementary route is to start from safe code and ask an instruction-tuned LLM to inject a specified CWE (which would shift the labeling burden from open-ended detection to bounded binary confirmation) but safety-aligned code LLMs systematically refuse such prompts. This paper is a preliminary feasibility study of abliteration, a low-rank weight edit that orthogonally projects out the refusal direction in the residual stream, as a tool to remove this barrier. We use Python and CWE-89 (SQL injection) as a case study, evaluating the Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct family at 3B, 7B, and 14B parameters on safe samples drawn from PromSec and SafeCoder, replicated three times per condition. We find that (i) refusal on injection prompts is strongly size- and prompt-context-dependent: the 14B refuses 100% of prompts, the 7B refuses 73% of PromSec but only 5% of SafeCoder, whereas the 3B is essentially never blocked; (ii) abliteration reduces refusal to zero or near-zero across all sizes while leaving syntactic validity above 93%, supporting the view that, in this setting, refusal can be detached from measured code-generation capability; and (iii) the post-abliteration injection rate remains capacity-bound (88-97% on the 14B, 89-90% on the 7B, and 25-48% on the 3B) separating willingness, which abliteration unlocks, from capability, which scales with parameters. Vulnerability verdicts are produced by a three-tool detector ensemble (CodeQL, Semgrep, Bandit) followed by manual adjudication by two authors on detector-positive outputs.

</details>


### [130] [CRESS: Quantifying Vulnerabilities of Attack Scenarios in Hardware Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05459)
*Alexander Hepp,Matthias Ludwig,Michaela Brunner,Johanna Baehr,Georg Sigl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并扩展了通用逆向工程评分系统（CRESS），通过专家访谈将原先的定性系统转化为量化系统，导出一个可计算严重性分数的公式，用于统一评估硬件逆向工程相关攻击场景，并通过六个案例验证其相比CVSS更具表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电子系统的安全依赖于可信的供应链与设计流程，但全球分布的供应链和设计漏洞导致硬件层面的攻击（如伪造、硬件木马、设备攻击）频发，而这些攻击离不开硬件逆向工程（RE）。现有的评分系统（如CVSS）不足以准确描述RE相关场景的严重性，因此需要一个专门针对RE场景的通用量化评分系统。

Method: 在原有的定性CRESS系统基础上，通过进行广泛的专家访谈研究，从访谈结果中推导出不同RE攻击类别的权重，形成一个量化方程，输出CRESS分数以指示场景的严重程度。

Result: 通过六个案例研究的定性与定量评估，CRESS分数被证明比行业标准的CVSS（通用漏洞评分系统）更具表现力和区分度，能够更准确地反映RE相关攻击场景的严重性。

Conclusion: 量化的CRESS系统能够实现对新型RE攻击场景的一致评分，使不同场景之间具有可比性，并为开发有效的防御措施提供支持，是CVSS的有力补充或替代方案。

Abstract: The safety, security, and reliability of microelectronic systems depend on a trustworthy, secured supply chain and design flow. Globally distributed supply chains or unintentional design weaknesses leave the door open for attacks on the hardware level. These scenarios encompass counterfeiting, hardware trojans, or on-device attacks. For these, hardware reverse engineering (RE) results play a pivotal role. The ongoing publication of new RE-involved attacks motivated the development of the common RE scoring system (CRESS). The system enables a general classification of RE-involved scenarios for a common, consistent rating. In this work, the originally qualitative system is extended to a quantitative system. We performed an extensive interview study with experts in the field. The interview results allowed us to derive weights that measure the severity of different RE-involved attack categories. The weights form an equation that quantifies scenarios, resulting in the severity-indicating CRESS score. The score enables the coherent rating of novel scenarios, renders them comparable, and supports the development of effective countermeasures. To showcase the effectiveness of the quantitative CRESS Score, six selected case studies are rated qualitatively and quantitatively. The CRESS Score proves to be significantly more expressive than the industry-standard Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS).

</details>


### [131] [SHIELDS: Automating OS Hardening with Iterative Multi-Agent Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05476)
*Andrew Hamara,Dwight Horne,Aldehir Rojas,Timothy Kurniawan,Sophie Lamothe,Vishal Suresh,Nicholas Turoci,Lawrence Wong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIELDS，一个基于多智能体LLM的系统，通过迭代反馈驱动的方式自动修复OS安全配置（如DISA STIG）违规，在多种虚拟机配置下成功修复了高达73%的扫描结果，并发现模型参数规模并非决定性因素，有效工具使用和信息收集更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全配置错误是OS级入侵的主要原因，而手动维护系统符合DISA STIG等安全标准既繁琐又昂贵。现有自动化合规工具虽然能减轻部分负担，但依赖静态、预写的修复措施，缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: 提出SHIELDS——一个多智能体系统，利用大型语言模型（LLM）将OS加固过程建模为迭代的、反馈驱动的流程。系统不是应用固定修复，而是持续提出修复方案，并根据目标系统执行结果和验证扫描的反馈不断优化这些方案。实验在多种虚拟机配置上使用6种从20B到400B参数的当代LLM进行评估。

Result: SHIELDS成功修复了高达73%的扫描发现项。结果还表明，在此场景下模型参数规模（参数量）对成功的贡献不如有效的工具使用和信息收集重要。

Conclusion: SHIELDS为减少安全合规负担提供了一条实用路径，特别是在计算资源受限或安全与隐私需求驱动使用本地模型的环境中。研究揭示了LLM在系统安全自动化中的新应用方向，即通过智能体协作和反馈迭代来替代静态脚本修复。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations remain a leading cause of OS-level compromise, and manually keeping systems compliant with standards like Defense Information Systems Agency (DISA) Security Technical Implementation Guides (STIGs) is a tedious and expensive process. Existing compliance automation tools can reduce some of this burden, but they depend on static, pre-written corrective actions. In this paper, we introduce SHIELDS, a multi-agent system that uses large language models (LLMs) to approach OS hardening as an iterative, feedback-driven process. Instead of applying fixed remediations, SHIELDS continuously proposes fixes and refines them based on feedback from target system execution and validation scans. We evaluate the system across multiple virtual machine configurations using six contemporary LLMs ranging from 20B to 400B parameters, and find that SHIELDS successfully remediates up to 73% of scan findings. Our results also suggest that success in this setting depends less on model size (parameter count) than on effective tool use and information gathering, paving a practical path toward reducing the burden of security compliance in environments where compute is limited or security and privacy needs drive local model use.

</details>


### [132] [Bitcoin After Block Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05503)
*Junhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币区块奖励归零后，矿工仅靠交易手续费时网络安全性问题。通过序贯决策模型识别诚实挖矿不再最优的偏离阈值Gt，发现2024年减半后尚无大规模偏离，但区块奖励移除后即使很小比例手续费也可能引发偏离。评估三种协议级机制（基础费、费用下限、自适应最大区块大小）组合能提高偏离阈值，缓解费用-only体制下的激励崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币区块奖励将持续递减至零，届时矿工仅依赖交易手续费维持网络运行，这引发了对网络长期安全性的担忧。作者试图识别在什么条件下大规模、持续性的偏离诚实挖矿行为可能出现。

Method: 采用序贯决策模型（sequential decision model），分析和比较诚实矿工与偏离矿工的收益，识别偏离阈值Gt。结合2024年比特币减半前后的实际数据进行验证，并评估三种协议级机制（Base Fee、Fee Floor、自适应最大区块大小规则）的效果。

Result: 2024年减半前后，当前挖矿行为未表现出大规模或结构性偏离；但区块奖励完全移除后，即使交易手续费占比极小，Gt准则也表明偏离可能发生；三种机制的组合能够提高偏离阈值，缓解激励崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 为评估比特币在区块奖励消失后的安全性提供了实用基准，指出了在纯费用体制下维持网络安全的机制设计方向。

Abstract: Bitcoin's block reward is scheduled to decline to zero, raising concerns about whether the network can remain secure once miners rely solely on transaction fees. This paper seeks to identify the conditions under which large-scale and persistent deviation from honest mining can arise.
  We analyze and compare the payoffs of honest and deviating miners in a sequential decision model, and identify a deviation threshold $G_t$ at which honest mining ceases to be privately optimal. Around the 2024 Bitcoin halving, we show that current mining behavior does not exhibit large-scale or structural deviation. However, when the block reward is removed, the $G_t$ criterion implies that deviation can arise even with a very small fraction of transaction fees.
  Finally, we evaluate three protocol-level mechanisms: Base Fee, Fee Floor, and an adaptive maximum block size rule, and show that their combination raises the deviation threshold and mitigates incentive breakdown in a fee-only regime. These results provide a practical benchmark for assessing Bitcoin's security as block rewards disappear.

</details>


### [133] [ZERO-APT: A Closed-Loop Adversarial Framework for LLM-Driven Automated Penetration Testing under Intelligent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05567)
*Anlan Zheng,Tiantian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZERO-APT是一个统一的攻击者-防御者-裁判框架，通过嵌入LLM驱动的实时防御系统、将因果一致性从不稳定的LLM推理转移到系统架构中（通过规划与执行分离、多维度ReAct反馈、硬约束过滤动作库），以及引入专门的裁判代理实现可审计性，从而解决了自动化渗透测试代理在真实性、一致性和可审计性方面的三大缺陷。在Windows Server 2022后渗透原型上的评估显示，攻击成功率79%（对比Aurora 22%、PentestGPT 39%），因果一致性得分0.860，并通过结构化CTI报告实现端到端决策可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动化渗透测试代理通常在静态、无防御的目标上评估，且攻击链的因果一致性依赖于不稳定的LLM推理，代理决策对分析人员不透明。这些真实性、一致性和可审计性的缺陷通常被孤立地修补，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出ZERO-APT框架，采用回合制的攻击者-防御者-裁判架构。(1) 真实性：嵌入可配置的LLM Defender，利用Sysmon遥测数据实时检测攻击。(2) 一致性：通过三种架构机制（规划与执行分离、多维度ReAct反馈、硬约束过滤动作库）将因果一致性从LLM推理转移到系统架构中。(3) 可审计性：专门的Judge代理对每回合进行裁决，维护全局状态，并生成结构化CTI报告。

Result: 在Windows Server 2022后渗透场景下，ZERO-APT达到79%的攻击成功率（Aurora 22%，PentestGPT 39%），因果一致性得分0.860（Aurora 0.930，Claude Code 0.520），并通过结构化CTI报告实现端到端决策可审计性。

Conclusion: ZERO-APT在单一架构中同时解决了自动化渗透测试代理的真实性、一致性和可审计性三大缺陷，证明了将因果一致性从LLM推理转移到系统架构设计的有效性，并发布了基准测试以支持在智能防御下的渗透代理评估。

Abstract: LLM-driven automated penetration testing agents are typically evaluated against static targets that neither detect nor respond to attacks, so their behavior under intelligent defense remains untested. The causal consistency of multi-step attack chains likewise hinges on unstable LLM reasoning, and agent decisions remain opaque to human analysts. These three shortcomings, in realism, consistency, and auditability, are usually patched in isolation. We present ZERO-APT, a turn-based attacker-defender-judge framework that addresses them within a single architecture. For realism, ZERO-APT embeds a configurable LLM Defender that consumes Sysmon telemetry and detects attacks in real time, exposing the attacker to a live opponent rather than a passive target. For consistency, three architectural mechanisms move causal consistency from unstable LLM reasoning into enforced system architecture: separation of planning from execution, multi-dimensional ReAct feedback, and a hard-constraint-filtered action library. For auditability, a dedicated Judge agent adjudicates each round, maintains global state, and emits structured post-hoc CTI reports that make every decision traceable. We evaluate a Windows Server 2022 post-exploitation prototype across five scenarios with three Defender configurations. ZERO-APT reaches 79\% attack success rate (Aurora 22\%, PentestGPT 39\%), a Causal Consistency Score of 0.860 (Aurora 0.930, Claude Code 0.520), and end-to-end decision auditability through structured CTI reports. We release the benchmark to support evaluation of penetration agents under intelligent defense.

</details>


### [134] [Dimensionality Reduction for Cyberattack Classification: A Comparative Evaluation of PCA and Linear Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05584)
*Nelly Elsayed,Zag ElSayed,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种降维方法（PCA和LPC）在网络攻击检测中的特征压缩效果，发现即使大幅压缩特征维度，PCA仍能保持分类性能，LPC稍逊但仍有竞争力，表明轻量级特征压缩可用于高效网络安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维特征表示在基于机器学习的网络攻击检测中广泛应用，但增加了计算复杂度，限制了在资源受限环境中的部署，因此需要研究有效的特征压缩技术。

Method: 对比了主成分分析（PCA）和线性预测编码（LPC）两种降维方法，生成不同维度的压缩特征表示，并在多个分类模型上进行评估。

Result: PCA在激进压缩下仍能保持分类性能；LPC提供了有竞争力的预测表示但性能退化略大；特征维度的大幅减少对分类精度影响极小。

Conclusion: 轻量级特征压缩（尤其是PCA）能够在不显著牺牲分类准确率的前提下大幅降低特征维度，适用于资源受限环境下的高效网络安全分析。

Abstract: High-dimensional feature representations are widely used in machine learning-based cyberattack detection systems. However, they increase computational complexity and may hinder deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we investigate feature compression techniques for cyberattack classification by comparing two dimensionality reduction approaches: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear Predictive Coding (LPC). Compressed feature representations with varying dimensionalities are generated and evaluated across several classification models. Experimental analysis demonstrates that PCA preserves classification performance even under aggressive compression. On the other hand, LPC provides competitive predictive representations with slightly larger performance degradation. The results show that substantial reductions in feature dimensionality can be achieved with minimal impact on classification accuracy, highlighting the potential of lightweight feature compression for efficient cybersecurity analytics.

</details>


### [135] [The Coverage Gap: Chile's Cyber Disclosure Framework versus the USA, EU and UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05594)
*David Mellafe Z*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"覆盖缺口"（Coverage Gap）概念，用以量化关键基础设施运营者的公开暴露与漏洞披露协调能力之间的差距。针对智利国家网络安全局（ANCI）依据第21.663号法律指定的915家"重要运营者"（OIVs），采用仅基于开源情报（OSINT）的被动方法进行全量普查。结果显示仅1.7%的OIV发布可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道，84%存在邮件认证配置错误，约23.5%使用已知漏洞组件。通过与美、英、荷、丹等国对比，智利整体落后约3-8年。作者提出四阶段路线图，并开源了工具anci-oiv-resolver。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施运营者的漏洞披露能力缺乏可量化的衡量标准，现有研究多依赖主动测试或自我声明，难以大规模审计。智利新颁布的第21.663号法律指定了915家OIV，但尚无系统性评估其安全披露准备情况的研究。

Method: 基于ISO/IEC 29147:2018和智利第21.459号法律（计算机犯罪安全港）原则，采用纯被动OSINT方法，对ANCI官方目录中约98.7%的OIV域名进行全量普查。检测指标包括：RFC 9116（security.txt）发布情况、DMARC/SPF/DKIM邮件认证配置、以及已知漏洞或EOL组件使用情况。跨司法管辖区对标采用文献对比法。

Result: 915家OIV中仅16家（1.7%）发布可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道；物理基础设施领域（能源、医疗、银行、通信等）不足10家，四大银行和两家电信巨头均无此配置。84%的OIV存在邮件认证配置错误（DMARC/SPF/DKIM不当）。约23.5%（Wilson 95% CI [12%,38%]）使用EOL或已知漏洞组件。智利在邮件认证规范上落后美国、英国、荷兰约8年，落后丹麦约3年。美国联邦文职机构在CISA BOD 18-01下合规率超99%。

Conclusion: 智利关键基础设施运营者的漏洞披露准备严重不足，亟需政策干预。作者提出四阶段路线图（参考BOD 18-01和英国公共部门DMARC工具包），并开源anci-oiv-resolver工具以支持独立复现和规模化审计，为政策制定和合规检查提供量化基础。

Abstract: We introduce the Coverage Gap as a measurable distance between the observable public exposure of critical-infrastructure operators and their declared capability to coordinate vulnerability disclosure. We instantiate it against the 915 Chilean Operadores de Importancia Vital (OIVs -- Operators of Vital Importance) designated by the National Cybersecurity Agency (ANCI) under Ley 21.663 (Resolucion Exenta No. 87, 16 December 2025). Using a passive-only, OSINT-based method consistent with the principles of ISO/IEC 29147:2018 and Chile's computer-crimes safe harbour (Ley 21.459), we conduct a full-universe census of the foundational disclosure-capability layer (Layer 1, verifiable disclosure contact) across approximately 98.7% of the official catalogue. Only 16 of 915 OIVs (1.7%) publish a verifiable RFC 9116 disclosure channel; among operators of physical-world infrastructure -- energy, health, banking, telecommunications, fuel, water, transport, and state administration -- fewer than ten do so, and all four major banks and both telecommunications incumbents lack one entirely. This compares with over 99% adherence in the U.S. federal civilian branch under CISA Binding Operational Directive 18-01. Email-authentication misconfiguration affects 766 of 915 (84%) OIVs, and end-of-life or known-vulnerable stack components an estimated 23.5% (Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]). Cross-jurisdictional benchmarking situates Chile roughly eight years behind the USA, the UK, and the Netherlands on email-authentication mandates, and three years behind Denmark. We propose a four-stage roadmap modelled on BOD 18-01 and the UK Public-Sector DMARC Toolkit, and release the open-source tool anci-oiv-resolver (Apache 2.0) to enable independent reproduction of the OIV-domain mapping that underpins universe-scale auditing.

</details>


### [136] [SlotGCG: Exploiting the Positional Vulnerability in LLMs for Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05609)
*Seungwon Jeong,Jiwoo Jeong,Hyeonjin Kim,Yunseok Lee,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotGCG方法，通过量化提示词中不同位置（slots）的脆弱性分数（VSS）来选择最脆弱的插入位置进行对抗性攻击，相比固定后缀攻击（GCG）实现了14%更高的攻击成功率，且仅需200ms预处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型越狱攻击（如GCG）仅将对抗性token固定插入在提示词末尾（后缀），忽视了其他插入位置可能带来的更好攻击效果。

Method: 提出脆弱性插槽分数（VSS）来量化提示词中各位置的越狱脆弱性；基于VSS选择最脆弱的插槽；在这些选定的插槽上运行针对性优化攻击（SlotGCG）。该方法与攻击无关，可作为插件集成到任何基于优化的攻击中。

Result: 在多个模型上，SlotGCG相比GCG基线方法攻击成功率提升14%；收敛速度更快；面对防御方法时攻击成功率高出42%；预处理时间仅增加200ms。

Conclusion: 提示词中token插入位置的选择对越狱攻击效果有显著影响，SlotGCG通过位置搜索机制有效提升了攻击效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are widely deployed, identifying their vulnerability through jailbreak attacks becomes increasingly critical. Optimization-based attacks like Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) have focused on inserting adversarial tokens to the end of prompts. However, GCG restricts adversarial tokens to a fixed insertion point (typically the prompt suffix), leaving the effect of inserting tokens at other positions unexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate \emph{slots}, i.e., candidate positions within a prompt where tokens can be inserted. We find that vulnerability to jailbreaking is highly related to the selection of the \emph{slots}. Based on these findings, we introduce the \textit{Vulnerable Slot Score} (VSS) to quantify the positional vulnerability to jailbreaking. We then propose SlotGCG, which evaluates all slots with VSS, selects the most vulnerable slots for insertion, and runs a targeted optimization attack at those slots. Our approach provides a position-search mechanism that is attack-agnostic and can be plugged into any optimization-based attack, adding only 200ms of preprocessing time. Experiments across multiple models demonstrate that SlotGCG significantly outperforms existing methods. Specifically, it achieves 14\% higher Attack Success Rates (ASR) over GCG-based attacks, converges faster, and shows superior robustness against defense methods with 42\% higher ASR than baseline approaches. Our implementation is available at \href{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}

</details>


### [137] [Protecting K-Nearest Neighbor Queries from Location Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05648)
*Zhiyu Sun,Jie Fu,Xinpeng Ling,Huifa Li,Zhili Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对k近邻查询（kNNQ）的两种位置推断攻击（几何交集攻击GI-LIA和零阶优化攻击ZO-LIA），并提出了差分隐私框架DPRS，通过带约束扰动区间的拒绝采样机制来平衡隐私保护与查询效用，实验表明DPRS优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k近邻查询（kNNQ）是LBS的核心组件（如"附近的人"），但其潜在隐私风险长期被忽视；现有方法未能有效平衡隐私保护与查询效用。

Method: 提出两种攻击方法：几何交集位置推断攻击（GI-LIA）和零阶优化位置推断攻击（ZO-LIA）；提出差分隐私框架DPRS，核心是在约束扰动区间内引入拒绝采样机制，并设计私有区间构造算法以优化隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在真实空间数据集上的实验表明，DPRS在隐私保护和查询效用两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: kNNQ存在固有位置隐私风险（通过两种攻击首次揭示），DPRS能有效缓解该风险，提供更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: The k-nearest neighbor query (kNNQ) is a core component of modern location-based services (LBS) and has been widely adopted in popular features such as ``people nearby''. However, its potential privacy risks have long been overlooked. In this work, we present the first two attacks against kNNQ, namely the geometric intersection location inference attack (GI-LIA) and the zero-order optimization location inference attack (ZO-LIA), revealing the inherent location privacy risks posed by kNNQ. To mitigate these privacy risks, we further propose DPRS, a differential privacy framework for kNNQ protection. The core idea of DPRS is to incorporate a rejection sampling mechanism within a constrained perturbation interval, thereby mitigating the distance distortion caused by excessive noise injection. In addition, we design a private interval construction algorithm to construct the perturbation interval, enabling the rejection sampling mechanism to achieve a more favorable trade-off between privacy protection and query utility in kNNQ. Extensive experiments on real-world spatial datasets demonstrate that DPRS outperforms existing methods in both privacy protection and query utility. Our code is available at https://github.com/reanatom/DPRS.

</details>


### [138] [Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics for distributed Infrastructure Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05701)
*Md. Arifur Rahman,B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Iqbal Hossan,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合联邦学习、可解释人工智能和认知网络安全分析的认知威胁情报与可解释联邦安全分析框架，用于分布式基础设施的隐私保护协同威胁检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式基础设施系统的普及扩大了攻击面，传统集中式入侵检测存在可扩展性差、数据隐私风险、通信开销高以及AI决策缺乏透明度等问题。

Method: 集成联邦学习（在分布式节点独立训练本地模型，仅共享加密模型参数）、可解释人工智能和认知网络安全分析，采用随机森林、XGBoost、自编码器等机器学习/深度学习算法。

Result: 框架在提升隐私保护的同时降低了通信依赖和集中式安全风险，实现了分布式网络环境下的协同威胁检测。

Conclusion: 联邦去中心化学习架构能有效解决传统集中式方法的局限性，为分布式基础设施提供更安全透明的威胁检测方案。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of distributed infrastructure systems, cloud computing, Internet of Things (IoT) technologies, and edge-based architectures has significantly expanded the cybersecurity attack surface and introduced increasingly sophisticated cyber threats. Conventional centralized intrusion detection approaches often face challenges related to scalability, data privacy, communication overhead, and limited transparency in artificial intelligence-driven decision-making processes. To address these limitations, this study proposes a Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics framework for distributed infrastructure systems. The proposed framework integrates Federated Learning (FL), Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), and cognitive cybersecurity analytics to enable collaborative and privacy-preserving cyber threat detection across distributed network environments. Instead of transmitting sensitive raw network traffic data to centralized servers, local security models are independently trained at distributed nodes, where only encrypted model parameters and updates are shared through a federated aggregation mechanism. This decentralized learning architecture improves privacy protection while reducing communication dependency and centralized security risks. To enhance intelligent threat analysis, the framework incorporates machine learning and deep learning algorithms including Random Forest, XGBoost, Autoencoder

</details>


### [139] [Explainable AI-Driven Cyber Risk Analytics and Model Reliability Assessment for Intelligent Governance of U.S. Critical Infrastructure: An XGBoost and SHAP-Based Intrusion Detection Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05710)
*B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Arifur Rahman,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Iqbal Hossan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对美国关键基础设施面临的日益复杂的网络威胁环境，提出了一种基于机器学习的弹性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架。该框架利用CICIDS2017数据集，结合XGBoost、随机森林和决策树等分类器进行入侵检测和风险预测，并集成可解释人工智能（XAI）技术提高决策透明度和可信度，通过准确率、精确率、召回率等多种性能指标评估模型可靠性与弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能数字技术在美国关键基础设施（能源、医疗、交通、金融、通信等领域）中的深度渗透，高级网络攻击（DDoS、僵尸网络、勒索软件、APT等）带来的威胁日益严峻，传统网络安全机制在动态网络环境中难以满足保护需求，亟需开发更具弹性和可信度的网络风险分析框架以支持智能治理与决策。

Method: 基于CICIDS2017数据集，采用XGBoost、随机森林和决策树等机器学习分类器进行恶意活动检测与网络风险等级预测，并集成可解释人工智能（XAI）技术提升决策过程的透明性与可解释性。通过准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC和假阳性率等指标评估模型性能。

Result: 提出了一套弹性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架，通过多种分类器实现了网络入侵检测与风险预测，XAI技术的集成增强了决策可解释性，各项性能指标（准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC、假阳性率）验证了模型的可靠性与弹性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效支持美国关键基础设施环境下的智能治理与网络风险决策，通过机器学习与XAI技术的融合，在提升检测能力的同时保障了决策过程的透明度与可信度。

Abstract: The increasing penetrations of the critical infrastructure sector in the United States with intelligent digital technologies have greatly increased exposure to advanced cyber adversaries and operational vulnerabilities. AI-powered governance and automated decision-making systems are becoming a key part of the operation of critical infrastructure systems, including energy, healthcare, transportation, financial services, and communication infrastructure, in order to improve efficiency and strategic management. The growing cyber threat environment, such as Distributed Denial of Service (DDos) attacks, botnets, ransomware, and Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) pose significant challenges to infrastructure resilience, cyber security reliability, and governance trustworthiness. In a changing attack landscape and dynamic network environment, traditional cybersecurity mechanisms can often fall short of meeting the evolving needs and protecting critical systems. This study will develop a resilient cyber risk analytics and model reliability assessment framework to support intelligent governance and decision support for cyber risk exposure in the U.S. critical infrastructure environment. This study is based on the CICIDS2017 dataset for the development and testing of intrusion detection system models and cyber risk prediction models based on machine learning. Various classifiers like XGBoost, Random Forest, and Decision Tree are used to detect malicious activities on the network and determine the level of cyber risk. Furthermore, the Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques are integrated to enhance transparency, interpretability, and trust in cybersecurity decision-making processes. The proposed framework presents the reliability and resilience of the model by having various performance measures such as accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, ROC-AUC, and false positive rate.

</details>


### [140] [Hybrid CNN-LSTM Framework for Intelligent Cyber Attack Detection and Prevention in U.S. Critical Digital Infrastructure: A Comparative Machine Learning Evaluation on CSE-CIC-IDS2018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05714)
*Md. Iqbal Hossan,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Arifur Rahman,B. M. Taslimul Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用AI/ML算法检测和防御美国数字基础设施网络攻击的智能网络防御系统，基于CSE-CIC-IDS2018数据集，评估了随机森林、XGBoost、CNN和LSTM等模型，通过数据预处理、特征工程、实时流量监控和自动威胁分类来提升入侵检测准确性和网络弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国数字基础设施快速扩张，传统基于签名的入侵检测系统无法有效检测未知和实时变化的网络攻击，亟需更智能的防御方法。

Method: 使用CSE-CIC-IDS2018真实网络流量数据集，涵盖DDoS、暴力破解、僵尸网络、渗透攻击和Web攻击等多种场景；实现并评估随机森林、XGBoost、CNN和LSTM等ML/DL模型；框架结合数据预处理、特征工程、实时流量监控、智能威胁分类和自动防御机制。

Result: 所提出的智能防御框架能够识别恶意网络行为，提升入侵检测准确性，增强网络安全弹性。

Conclusion: AI/ML驱动的智能防御系统可有效克服传统方法的局限性，为美国关键基础设施提供更先进的网络攻击防护能力。

Abstract: Digital infrastructure is growing at a rapid pace in the United States, and as a result, exposure to advanced cyber threats to critical sectors including healthcare, finance, transportation, energy and government systems is growing. The traditional cybersecurity approaches, including signature-based intrusion detection systems, have become less effective against today's cyber attacks, as they are unable to detect unknown and changing attacks in real time. To overcome these constraints, this research suggests a smart cyber-defense system, which utilizes Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) algorithms in the detection and prevention of cyber attacks in the U.S. digital infrastructure. This study uses the CSE-CIC-IDS2018 dataset, which is a realistic network traffic dataset, along with various cyber attack scenarios, including Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS), brute force attacks, botnets, infiltration attacks, and web-based attacks. A number of machine learning and deep learning models such as Random Forest, XGBoost, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks are implemented and evaluated to be used in identifying malicious network behavior and boosting the accuracy of intrusion detection. The framework proposed combines data preprocessing, feature engineering, real-time traffic monitoring, intelligent threat classification with automated prevention mechanisms to build cybersecurity resilience. E

</details>


### [141] [An Embarrassingly Simple Detector for Model Extraction Attacks in Large Language Model API Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05725)
*Shuze Liu,Qianwen Guo,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于良性校准的流量窗口分布测试方法用于LLM模型窃取检测，通过MMD指标比较查询语义分布与历史良性流量，在多种攻击场景下达到95.1%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过托管API部署面临模型窃取威胁，现有检测方法多聚焦单查询异常评分或纯良性vs攻击者设定，缺乏对混合多用户场景下分布级检测的研究。

Method: 将查询嵌入语义空间，通过最大均值差异(MMD)比较聚合分布与历史良性流量；仅使用良性vs良性比较设定决策阈值。

Result: 在14个攻击者-正常查询对上，MMD达到0.3%良性FPR、100.0%纯攻击者TPR、90.5%平均TPR、95.1%平衡准确率，优于PRADA、SEAT、CAP、DATE和边际马氏距离基线。

Conclusion: 良性校准的分布测试是LLM API流量中模型窃取检测的强经验基线，适用于用户级和混合多用户场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed through hosted APIs, making model extraction a practical threat to model ownership and service security. However, individual extraction queries often resemble benign requests, and existing evaluations often focus on single-query anomaly scoring or pure benign-versus-attacker user settings. We formulate model extraction monitoring as benign-calibrated traffic-window distribution testing and show that an embarrassingly simple detector is effective: embed incoming queries into a semantic space and test whether their aggregate distribution deviates from historical benign traffic. We instantiate the detector with maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), using only benign-vs-benign comparisons to set the decision threshold. We evaluate on fourteen attacker-normal query pairs from four extraction scenarios and compare with adapted PRADA, SEAT, CAP, DATE, and marginal Mahalanobis baselines. Across three random seeds, MMD achieves 0.3% benign FPR, 100.0% pure-attacker TPR, 90.5% average TPR over attacker fractions, and 95.1% balanced accuracy. These results show that benign-calibrated distribution testing is a strong empirical baseline for model extraction detection in both user-level and mixed multi-user LLM API traffic. Code is released at: https://github.com/LabRAI/mmd-llm-mea-detection.

</details>


### [142] [Membrane: A Self-Evolving Contrastive Safety Memory for LLM Agent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05743)
*Minseok Choi,Seungbin Yang,Dongjin Kim,Subin Kim,Jungmin Son,Yunseung Lee,Jaegul Choo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Membrane是一种基于对比安全记忆(CSM)的自进化护栏，通过为每种攻击策略建立对比细胞（同时编码拒绝有害查询和允许相似良性查询的条件），在不重训练的情况下持续适应新越狱攻击，在HarmBench和AgentHarm上取得最高F1分数，同时大幅降低良性误拒率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法中，微调的安全分类器无法适应不断演化的越狱攻击，而基于记忆的护栏容易过度拒绝与存储攻击相似的良性查询，因此需要一种既能自适应新型攻击又保持低良性误拒率的防护机制。

Method: 提出Membrane框架，核心是对比安全记忆(CSM)，每个记忆细胞配对存储拒绝有害查询的条件和允许表面相似良性查询的条件。无需重训练，通过将每次有害交互及其良性对照蒸馏为对比细胞，并以底层攻击策略索引，使单个细胞可泛化到同一机制的不同主题变体。推理时检索相关细胞作为上下文进行精确安全决策。

Result: 在模型级安全(HarmBench)和智能体级安全(AgentHarm)上，Membrane在所有六种越狱攻击中取得最高F1分数；AgentHarm上良性误拒率仅7-14%，远低于先前方法的28-85%；跨攻击迁移下记忆细胞保持87-88% F1，且在记忆中毒攻击下保持稳定。

Conclusion: Membrane通过对比安全记忆机制，在不重训练的情况下实现了对新型越狱攻击的自适应防护，同时显著降低了良性查询的误拒率，在跨攻击迁移和对抗鲁棒性方面也表现优异。

Abstract: Despite advances in safety alignment, large language models remain vulnerable to continuously evolving jailbreaks. Existing fine-tuned safety classifiers cannot adapt to these evolving attacks, while adaptive memory-based guardrails tend to over-refuse benign queries that resemble stored attacks. We propose Membrane, a self-evolving guardrail built on Contrastive Safety Memory (CSM): each cell pairs the conditions for blocking a harmful query with those for permitting a superficially similar benign request. Without retraining, Membrane evolves CSM by distilling each harmful interaction and its benign counterpart into a contrastive cell indexed by the underlying attack strategy, so that one cell generalizes across topical variants of the same mechanism. At inference, retrieved cells serve as grounding context for precise safety decisions. Across model-level safety on HarmBench and agent-level safety on AgentHarm, Membrane achieves the highest F1 on all six jailbreak attacks. Notably, benign refusal on AgentHarm stays at 7-14%, well below the 28-85% range of prior guards. Memory cells also retain 87-88% F1 under cross-attack transfer and remain stable under memory poisoning.

</details>


### [143] [An Improved CNN-LSTM Based Intrusion Detection System for IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05776)
*Mohammad Tariq Ikhlas,Pohanyar Khowaja Khil,Malik Muhammad Mueed Aslam,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的CNN-LSTM入侵检测模型，结合多分类、数据集集成和时序特征学习，在物联网网络流量数据上实现了约97%的准确率，能有效检测多种攻击类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备快速普及导致安全威胁急剧增加，亟需增强入侵检测系统在物联网环境中的检测性能。

Method: 提出改进的CNN-LSTM模型，结合卷积神经网络（捕获空间特征）和循环神经网络（捕获时间特征），采用多分类方法并集成数据集，对网络流量数据进行时序特征学习。

Result: 在入侵检测任务上达到约97%的准确率，训练和验证性能稳定，能有效检测多种攻击类别。

Conclusion: CNN与LSTM的集成使模型能够同时捕获网络流量的空间和时间特征，显著提升了物联网环境下的入侵检测能力。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of IoT devices, security concerns have dramatically escalated and intrusion detection systems have become critical for protecting networked environments. This paper presents an improved CNN-LSTM based intrusion detection model that combines multi-class classification, dataset integration, and temporal feature learning to enhance detection performance in IoT networks. Using network traffic data, the proposed approach is evaluated on intrusion detection tasks and achieves an accuracy of approximately 97%. Experimental results demonstrate that the model effectively detects multiple attack categories while maintaining stable training and validation performance. The integration of convolutional and recurrent neural network components enables the framework to capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of network traffic, improving overall intrusion detection capability in IoT environments.

</details>


### [144] [TinyML-Driven Cybersecurity for Autonomous Spacecraft: Latency-Accuracy Analysis for SPARTA RF and Cyber Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05779)
*Van Le,Trevor Tran,Tan Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在航天器上使用TinyML兼容的经典机器学习模型（随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM、MLP）检测射频网络威胁的时延-精度权衡，发现逻辑回归在微秒级推理下仅比随机森林低1%精度，适合作为星载自主基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器需要快速、轻量且可靠的在轨射频威胁检测能力，现有方法在计算资源受限的航天环境中难以同时满足实时性和准确性要求。

Method: 基于SPARTA攻击模型，对随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM和MLP四种TinyML兼容模型进行物理信息驱动的理论分析（计算复杂度、VC维、Lipschitz连续性、时延缩放），并在通过BandErasure、FakeNR和NoiseBurst三种对抗性RF频谱图上进行实证测量。

Result: 逻辑回归在微秒级推理时延下仅比随机森林低1%精度；通过理论分析和实证测量揭示了各模型在时延-精度权衡中的表现差异。

Conclusion: 逻辑回归是星载自主威胁检测的有效TinyML基线；未来可通过更丰富的特征编码器和多时间尺度学习架构进一步提升航天器网络安全性能。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft require rapid, lightweight, and reliable onboard detection of cyber-RF threats. Using the SPARTA attack model, we analyze the latency-accuracy trade-offs of TinyML-compatible classical models -- Random Forest, Logistic Regression, SVM, and MLP -- for detecting uplink jamming, Fake-NR spoofing, payload manipulation, ground-segment compromise, and unauthorized command injection. We present a physics-informed theoretical analysis of each model's computational complexity, VC dimension, Lipschitz continuity, and latency scaling, supported by empirical measurements on adversarial RF spectrograms generated via BandErasure, FakeNR, and NoiseBurst corruption modes. Results show that Logistic Regression achieves microsecond-level inference with only a 1\% accuracy drop relative to Random Forest, making it an effective TinyML baseline for onboard autonomy. The study also identifies opportunities for advancing spacecraft cybersecurity through richer feature encoders and multi-timescale learning architectures, building on recent progress in edge intelligence and trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [145] [SentinelRAG: Synthetic Sentinel Knowledge for RAG Database Copyright Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05787)
*Tsun On Kwok,Xi Yang,Ki Sen Hung,Chang Liu,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SentinelRAG提出了一种面向RAG数据库的版权水印框架，通过注入风格一致但虚构的知识条目，在仅0.1%的注入率下实现统计显著的水印检测（p<10^{-5}），同时保持对合法查询的极小干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG数据库水印方法存在两大缺陷：一是注入实体间虚假关系会污染知识库并引入错误信息；二是嵌入脆弱词汇模式易被对抗性改写去除。亟需一种既不影响数据库正常使用又能可靠检测盗版的水印方案。

Method: SentinelRAG的核心思想是将描述虚构实体的合成知识条目注入RAG数据库。这些条目与数据库风格一致，但由于描述的是虚构实体，合法查询几乎不会检索到它们；只有掌握特定探针（probes）的数据所有者才能触发这些水印条目的检索，从而实现版权检测。

Result: 在4个数据集（2.9k~8.8M文档）上的实验表明：SentinelRAG在仅0.1%注入率下，所有配置均达到统计显著检测水平（p<10^{-5}）；与现有最优方法相比，显著降低了误检测率，且对合法用户查询的干扰可忽略不计。

Conclusion: SentinelRAG通过注入虚构知识条目实现了高可靠、低干扰的RAG数据库水印方案，有效解决了现有方法污染知识库或脆弱易去除的困境。

Abstract: Protecting proprietary RAG databases from unauthorized redistribution is challenging: existing watermarking methods either inject fabricated relations between real entities, polluting the knowledge base with misinformation, or embed fragile lexical patterns that adversarial paraphrasing easily removes. We propose SentinelRAG, a watermarking framework that embeds style-consistent but fictitious knowledge entries into the RAG database. Our key insight is that synthetic knowledge describing fictitious entities is unlikely to be retrieved by legitimate queries, yet can be reliably triggered through targeted probes known only to the data owner. Experiments on four datasets ranging from 2.9k to 8.8M documents demonstrate that SentinelRAG achieves statistically significant detection $p < 10^{-5}$ across all tested configurations at only a 0.1% injection rate. Compared to the state-of-the-art, our method significantly reduces the false detection rate while maintaining negligible interference with legitimate user queries.

</details>


### [146] [GCD: Garbled, Corrected, Demonstrandum -- Fixing and Proving Go's Extended GCD Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05796)
*Linard Arquint*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文验证了Go标准库中'extendedGCD'的实现，发现两个偏离BoringSSL的缺陷，通过修复和证明扩展解决了这些问题，同时揭示了形式化验证和AI辅助在发现细微bug中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go标准库中的'extendedGCD'实现是RSA密钥对生成的关键组件，尽管声称是BoringSSL的直接移植，但仍可能存在细微的、破坏算法不变量的偏差，需要通过形式化验证来确保其正确性。

Method: 使用Gobra（Go的演绎程序验证器）对修复后的实现进行正确性和终止性证明；对于非线性算术的关键引理，使用Lean辅助证明并导入Gobra；AI代理通过迭代优化Gobra错误信息中的不变量和引理来辅助验证过程。

Result: 发现两个偏差：系数更新方式偏离和输入域过大；修复后实现平均加速24%；成功证明了修复后实现的正确性和终止性。

Conclusion: 即使经过充分审查的代码也可能存在细微缺陷，形式化验证是发现此类问题的有力工具，AI代理可以通过迭代优化验证过程中的不变量和引理来加速形式化验证。

Abstract: We verify the 'extendedGCD' implementation in Go's standard library ('crypto/internal/fips140/bigmod'), which plays a crucial role in the generation of RSA key pairs. Even though the Go implementation is supposedly a direct port from BoringSSL's implementation, we uncovered two deviations that each break the algorithm's invariants: (1) the Go implementation deviates in the way coefficients are updated, and (2) it permits a larger input domain. We address both deviations; the first by fixing the Go implementation, which results in an on average 24% speedup, and the second deviation by porting an existing proof for BoringSSL and extending it to cover the larger input domain. We prove correctness and termination of the fixed Go implementation using Gobra, a deductive program verifier for Go. Where necessary, we used Lean to prove key lemmata on non-linear arithmetic, which we import into Gobra.
  Our verification effort reveals three key insights: subtle bugs can slip into even well-reviewed code with surprising ease; formal verification is a powerful tool for uncovering them; and AI agents can facilitate the verification process by iteratively refining invariants and lemmata based on Gobra's error messages.

</details>


### [147] [PriSrv: Privacy-Enhanced and Highly Usable Service Discovery in Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05821)
*Yang Yang,Robert H. Deng,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,HweeHwa Pang,Minming Huang,Rui Shi,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PriSrv——一种基于匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）的私有服务发现协议，通过双层匹配架构实现细粒度认证策略和隐私保护，在主流移动设备上可在1秒内完成安全发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议隐私保护有限，容易泄露敏感设备信息并招致网络攻击。

Method: 设计双层匹配架构（外层过滤不匹配实体，内层处理双向认证）；提出匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）原语，实现单步双层匹配、双边策略控制、选择性属性披露和多展示不可链接性；设计快速匿名凭证（FAC）方案提供常数大小凭证和高效验证。

Result: 形式化安全证明和跨平台（桌面、笔记本、智能手机、树莓派）性能评估表明，PriSrv在主流移动设备上可在1秒内完成安全发现，并已集成到EAP、mDNS、BLE、AirDrop等无线框架中。

Conclusion: PriSrv在增强隐私保障的同时保持了高可用性，为无线服务发现提供了实用的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Service discovery is essential in wireless communications. However, existing protocols provide limited privacy protection, leaking sensitive device information and opening routes to network attacks. This paper proposes a private service discovery protocol, called PriSrv, which enables both service providers and clients to specify fine-grained authentication policies before establishing connections. PriSrv achieves this via a dual-layer matching architecture: an outer layer filters mismatched entities using public attributes, while an inner layer handles mutual authentication using selectively disclosed private attributes. As a core component, we introduce the primitive of anonymous credential-based matchmaking encryption (ACME), which enables dual-layer matching in a single step to achieve bilateral policy control, selective attribute disclosure, and multi-show unlinkability. To instantiate ACME, we design a fast anonymous credential (FAC) scheme providing constant-size credentials and efficient verification. We demonstrate PriSrv's interoperability by integrating it with popular wireless frameworks including EAP, mDNS, BLE, and AirDrop. Detailed formal security proofs and extensive performance evaluations across desktop, laptop, smartphone, and Raspberry Pi platforms demonstrate that PriSrv provides enhanced privacy guarantees with high usability, achieving secure discovery in less than one second on mainstream mobile devices.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Worst-case Hardness for Low-Noise LPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05834)
*Divesh Aggarwal,Rishav Gupta,Hai Hoang Nguyen,Kel Zin Tan,Prashant Nalini Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的最坏情况到平均情况归约方法，通过将统计光滑性条件替换为计算不可区分性，首次在逆多项式噪声率下建立了LPN问题的平均情况困难性，并实现了Alekhnovich公钥加密方案所需参数范围内的LPN困难性归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPN问题是密码学的基础假设，但能否像LWE那样基于最坏情况复杂性假设建立其平均情况困难性一直是一个核心开放问题。现有归约方法依赖线性码的统计光滑性，只能达到$1/2 - 1/\text{poly}(n)$的噪声率，不足以支持公钥加密应用。

Method: 将生成矩阵随机稀疏行组合的统计均匀性要求替换为计算不可区分性要求，构建一个赢-赢结构：证明任意高效LPN求解器可转化为一对高效算法(S, D)，其中S解码A生成的码，或D区分对偶码的随机噪声码字与均匀分布。通过适当参数实例化该归约，得到逆多项式噪声率$n^{-α}$下的平均情况LPN困难性。

Result: 对于任意常数α<1，在同时假设最坏情况下解码码字与区分对偶码噪声码字的困难性时，得到逆多项式噪声率$n^{-α}$下LPN的平均情况困难性。特别地，设α=1/2时，归约得到Alekhnovich公钥加密方案所需参数范围内的LPN困难性，该参数范围此前无法通过最坏情况归约达到。

Conclusion: 通过用计算不可区分性替代统计光滑性，本文突破了现有最坏情况到平均情况归约的噪声率瓶颈，为LPN在公钥加密中的应用提供了新的理论基础，展示了计算光滑性在密码学归约中的潜力。

Abstract: The hardness of the Learning Parity with Noise (LPN) problem is a foundational assumption in cryptography, forming the basis of constructions ranging from symmetric-key primitives to public-key encryption and beyond. A central open question is whether the average-case hardness of LPN can be based on worst-case complexity assumptions, as has been achieved for the analogous Learning With Errors (LWE) problem.
  Existing worst-case-to-average-case reductions for LPN [BLVW19, YZ21] rely on statistical smoothing of linear codes, which inherently limits the resulting average-case hardness to noise rates as large as $1/2 - 1/\mathrm{poly}(n)$, which is insufficient for public-key applications.
  We explore a new approach towards obtaining such reductions: rather than requiring that random sparse combinations of the rows of the generator matrix of a code be statistically close to uniform, we only require that they be computationally indistinguishable from uniform.
